# Dali: economy, fitness market and the Ashtanga shala model

**No. I would not open, own or license a shala in Dali, because no rented room pays in any demand case.**

Written on Mon 23 Nov 2026 for Boonchu, AYBKK. It builds on yesterday's Dali note ("Dali Yoga Fitness Ashtanga Research", 22 Nov 2026) and does not repeat it. Dali is 大理, in Yunnan, China. Not for posting: it holds the yearly goal and income sums.

## 0. The answer on one page

To license means to let someone use the name and method for a fee. A demand case is one guess at how many people would pay: low, base (the middle guess) or high.

If a shala runs there at all, it has one shape. A graduate who already lives in Dali teaches at home, on **6 mats**. The one daily Mysore room in Dali already has that form. AYBKK cannot own such a room, and I would keep the AYBKK name off it.

**What you asked, in one table.** All rows are ESTIMATES from the model [S248]. Most inputs are guesses. Each guess is marked in sections 2 to 6.

| You asked | Answer (low / base / high where three numbers show) |
|---|---|
| Should a shala run in Dali? | No, as an AYBKK room. On every term found, a rented room loses money. |
| What size? | Of the three sizes asked for, small loses least: 10 mats, 30 m2 of practice floor, 53 m2 in all, serving 16 month members. If a room runs at all, it is smaller: **6 mats** (5 to 8), 18 m2 of practice floor, 32 m2 in all, serving 9 month members, in the teacher's own home. |
| What area? | None is proven. The foothill belt beside the Old Town, at the Shanshuijian gates, scores highest: 50 of 75. Its cheap spaces are homes. Of the areas with the right people, only the Old Town inside the walls has a unit that can hold a business licence at a known asking rent: 39 of 75. |
| Income of the owner, small rented room | Pay plus result: -11,425 / **-9,035** / -1,332 CNY a month. That is -137,095 / **-108,423** / -15,978 CNY a year. |
| Income of the teacher, home room of 6 mats | The teacher keeps -329 / **2,061** / 9,553 CNY a month (-1,665 / 10,440 / 48,398 THB). That is -3,944 / **24,729** / 114,641 CNY a year. |
| Income of AYBKK | **0 CNY or a loss** on days you do not teach, in every case. A workshop you teach there, in a hall (1 a year at low and base, 2 at high): 11,358 / **16,377** / 80,724 CNY a year, on an example 70/30 split. Base is 82,964 THB, or about 1,365 CNY a month (16,377 / 12). |
| Income of AYBKK if it owns the room | -263,809 / **-230,117** / -101,496 CNY a year. Base is about -19,176 CNY a month (230,117 / 12). |
| Who owns it? | Nobody under the AYBKK name. You through a company: no. A graduate on a licence: no, the law blocks it. A partner split: no. |
| On what terms would it be yes? | Three things at once. The town's count of month members proves well above the 19.4 a small room needs, and the teacher of the existing Mysore room says a second room is welcome. A unit that can hold a licence turns up at a rent those heads can carry. A lawyer says yes on the work rule and on the name. None is true today. |

A month member is a person on a month pass. "Result" is what is left after the teacher's pay of 6,000 CNY a month (30,396 THB) and after tax.

**Where the skeptics changed the answer.** The first model said a small room would make 21,838 CNY a year at base. Three skeptics then tested it: one on demand, one on cost and law, one on strategy. The revised model says the same room loses **180,423 CNY** a year. Base demand fell from 6.0 month members to 2.4. The rent now comes from a real advert. The licence case went from 60,395 CNY a year to 0, because the law blocks it. Section 5 shows each change.

**One action:** send the message that is already in yesterday's plan, step 2(b), by Sun 29 Nov 2026. It asks the teacher of the Dali Mysore room how many practise each morning, and if a visit is welcome. That number would replace most of the guesses in this note. No trip, no money.

**Stakes:** a small rented room costs 196,351 CNY to open. Then it loses about 180,423 CNY a year at base, which is 914,021 THB or 5.1 seats of the 180K course (ESTIMATE) [S248]. Cohort 3 starts in 49 days, and the week from Mon 16 Nov had brought 0 new 180K leads by 21 Nov (FACT) [S249].

**Why care:** the belief is that a shala in Dali could earn on days you do not teach. In this model every case earns 0 or a loss on those days. One more paid seat in cohort 3 is 35,531 CNY with no extra days. That equals 2.2 Dali trips (ESTIMATE: 35,531 / 16,377) [S248].

**The 5 numbers that matter**

| # | Number | What it is | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1,300 CNY a month (6,586 THB) | Mysore-only month price at the one listed room in Dali City. The month price has not risen since 2023. The week card fell from 900 to 600 and the single class from 200 to 150. | FACT, Feb 2026. The room's own notices, read in previews. | [S61][S62] |
| 2 | 2.4 month members | Base-case demand for a new daily room in Dali (low 0.3, high 8.1). Of its 2.2 settled places, 1.2 come out of rooms that exist. | ESTIMATE built on guesses: 0.2 x 6 + 1.0 = 2.2 places; x 87.5% = 1.93 paying; + 0.44 long-stayers = 2.4 | [S248] |
| 3 | 19.4 against 16 | Month members a small rented room needs to pay its teacher 6,000 CNY a month, against the 16 it can serve. | ESTIMATE: 215,799 / 11,133 = 19.4 | [S248] |
| 4 | -180,423 CNY a year (-914,021 THB) | Result of a small rented room at base demand. Cash to open it: 196,351 CNY (994,714 THB, 5.5 seats). | ESTIMATE | [S248] |
| 5 | 2,340 against 14,212 CNY a day | What AYBKK nets per day away from one Dali workshop of 7 students, on an example 70/30 split, against one cohort teaching day before costs. | ESTIMATE: 16,377 / 7 days; 72,000 THB / 5.066 | [S248][S134] |

**The 3 biggest risks**

1. No spare demand. The town's only daily Mysore room has cut its hours and two of its three prices. A new room fills by taking that teacher's members. Nobody has asked the teacher there.
2. No legal space where the right people live. The cheap spaces are homes. A home needs the neighbours' consent for business use, and the fire step before opening cannot be done there.
3. The rules on work and on names. Visa-free entry does not cover work. A ban would end every China stop. A franchise is a deal where one business lets another trade under its name and method for a fee. A name plus a method plus a fee is treated as one. That needs 2 shops you run yourself. AYBKK has 1.

## How to read this note

- **One note per topic.** This is part of the one Dali note, together with yesterday's market research.
- **Places.** Dali City (大理市) is the small city: Xiaguan (the modern centre), the Old Town and the lake villages. Dali prefecture (大理州) is the big area around it: Dali City plus 11 counties. Dali Town (大理镇) is the part of Dali City that holds the Old Town and the foothill estates. Each number says which place it is for.
- **Tags.** FACT: an official page, a law text, the press or a published price says it. VENDOR CLAIM: a business, a paid report or a self-media page says it. ESTIMATE: a sum, with the sum shown. "Guess" marks a model input with no evidence behind it. The model has 118 inputs: 29 facts, 11 vendor claims, 10 estimates and 68 guesses [S248].
- **Sources.** [S5] means source 5 in the list at the end. Numbers S1 to S144 are yesterday's and keep their numbers. New ones start at S145.
- **Money.** Prices are in CNY (Chinese yuan). Key numbers also show Thai baht at 1 CNY = 5.066 THB [S133]. One seat of the 180K course is 180,000 THB, or 35,531 CNY, before 7% VAT (a sales tax).
- **The model.** A model is a set of sums that turns guesses into money. All model numbers here are copied from its output file [S248]. Low, base and high are three sets of guesses.
- **Limits.** Every rent and every home price is an asking price from an advert. No paid rent was found. WeChat prices were read in public search previews, not in full posts. No Dali room publishes its class size. Law texts were read on copies. Open the source before any number goes public.
- **Missing numbers.** A number only you hold is written [Boonchu to fill: name]. A number nobody could find is written "not found".

## 1. Dali's economy in short

The short version: the local economy is weak in 2026 and local pay is low. The only growing group that could pay a Mysore price is long-stay people from other cities. That last line is my reading of the tables below.

### Size and growth

| What | Figure | Place | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Yearly output (GDP, the value of everything made and sold in a year) | 63.288 billion CNY, up 3.2% | Dali City | FACT, 2025 | [S5][S6] |
| Where it comes from | Farming 4.8%, industry and building 26.6%, services 68.6% | Dali City | FACT, 2025 | [S5] |
| Services; industry and building | Up 4.9%; down 0.8% | Dali City | FACT, 2025 | [S5] |
| Shop sales | 31.499 billion CNY, down 0.4% | Dali City | FACT, 2025 | [S5] |
| Money put into buildings and kit | Down 23.5%. Property alone down 26.2%. | Dali City | FACT, 2025 | [S5] |
| Output per person | About 82,000 CNY a year | Dali City | ESTIMATE: 63.288 billion / 771,128 people = 82,072 | [S5][S2] |
| Yearly output | 208.751 billion CNY, up 4.1%. Per person: 62,678 CNY. | Dali prefecture | FACT, 2025 | [S3] |
| First half of 2026 | 98.529 billion CNY, up 2.5%. Shop sales down 1.8%. Services up 3.2%. | Dali prefecture | FACT, Jan to Jun 2026 | [S145] |
| January to April 2026 | Shop sales down 1.4%. Added value of larger factories down 2.2%. | Dali prefecture | FACT, Jan to Apr 2026 | [S146] |
| Visitors' spending | 94.67 billion CNY on 78.08 million visits | Dali City | FACT, 2025 | [S6] |
| Visitors' spending against output | 1.5 times | Dali City | ESTIMATE: 94.67 / 63.288 = 1.50. A size mark only: spending is takings, output is added value. | [S6][S5] |
| Spending per visit | About 1,560 CNY in 2024, about 1,147 in 2025 | Dali prefecture | ESTIMATE: 171.66 billion / 110.04 million; 139.355 billion / 121.49 million | [S3][S4] |
| Household bank savings | 200.483 billion CNY, up 9.01%, while shop sales rose 2.4% | Dali prefecture | FACT, end 2025 | [S3] |
| People | 3.321 million. Births 18,200, deaths 27,100 in the year. | Dali prefecture | FACT, 2025 | [S3] |
| People | 771,128 in all. Xiaguan side 395,041. Dali Town 95,442. | Dali City | FACT, 2020 census (old) | [S2] |

### Incomes and pay

| What | Figure (CNY) | Place | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Income per person in town households | 51,005 a year, about 4,250 a month | Dali City | FACT, 2025. Month: ESTIMATE, 51,005 / 12. | [S5] |
| Income per person, all households | 42,341 a year, about 3,528 a month | Dali City | FACT, 2025. Month: ESTIMATE, 42,341 / 12. | [S5] |
| Income per person in village households | 27,046 a year | Dali City | FACT, 2025 | [S5] |
| Income and spending per person, all households | 30,918 and 25,562 a year | Dali prefecture | FACT, 2025 | [S3] |

A transfer fee is money paid to the tenant who leaves, to take over the lease (the rent contract). Rent is most often paid a year ahead, and in some adverts two years ahead [S169][S170].

## 2. The health and fitness market

### The places and their prices

All prices are for places in Dali City. The tai chi school's address is not shown. "Old" means 2023 or earlier.

How many places there are:

- A public map shows 13 to 15 yoga or Pilates places in Dali City, by search word. Ten are on the Old Town side and three in Xiaguan. Six of the ten are real local studios. None is proven open (FACT, read 23 Nov 2026) [S175].
- The same map shows about 15 gyms. It shows nothing for indoor climbing, CrossFit, Muay Thai or street dance (FACT, one map; that is not proof that none exists) [S175]. Four gyms and a dance studio sit at the Shanshuijian gates.
- The local business directory lists 0 yoga studios (FACT) [S200]. A company registry counted 61 yoga-related businesses in "Dali" in Mar 2023, place not clear (FACT, old) [S38].
- Across China, about one small fitness studio in five closed in 2025 in the main cities (VENDOR CLAIM, a software firm's data, not yoga, not Dali) [S101].

A Dali Mysore month costs 2.2 to 2.6 times the old general yoga card (ESTIMATE: 1,300 / 580 and 1,500 / 580). A Dali month at 1,300 CNY is 68.6% of the Bangkok 1 month pass (ESTIMATE: 1,300 / 1,895). The two Bangkok price lists in the files do not agree. Only the 1 month pass is used here, because it is the same in both.

### The two Ashtanga rooms, and a third that went quiet

**The listed room** (FACT, the room's own notices, previews) [S61][S62][S186][S187]:

- It sits in the back yard of a ground-floor home in a housing estate. Room size, mats and head count are not published.
- Times. 2023: 7:55 to 10:25. 2024: 6:30 to 9:30. 2026: breathing class 8:30 to 9:00, Mysore 9:00 to 11:00, teacher in the room 9:00 to 10:45.
- So the teaching window went from 3 hours in 2024 (the first half hour was free sitting) to 1 hour 45 in 2026. The start moved from 6:30 to 9:00.
- Month price: 1,530 (2023), 1,300 to 1,400 (2024), 1,500 with the breathing class or 1,300 without (2026).
- Week card: 900 (2023), 600 (2026). Single class: 200 (2023), 160 (2024), 150 (2026).
- The 2026 notice prints a term of 4 Jan to 3 Jul. Nothing was found for July to December 2026.
- The newest notice found is dated 3 Mar 2026. A thin search record is not proof that it closed.

**The lakeside courtyard** (FACT, previews) [S63][S64][S65]:

- 13 dated posts from Nov 2020 to Feb 2026. Twelve sell courses or retreats. One is a daily class notice.
- That notice (26 Mar 2024): Mysore Sunday to Thursday 7:00 to 10:00, week card 600, single class 120. No month price.
- Its 100-hour course listed at 12,880 CNY with a cap of 20, and 7,580 for the first ten (Nov 2023). Nothing shows that a course filled.

**The third yard**, in Shengjiu village (FACT, old) [S188]:

- In Nov 2022 its owner wrote of "a yoga class of two" and "a Mysore of ten". That is the only head count for a Mysore class in Dali.
- Last trace: one event in May 2024.

Three more studios have put Ashtanga on a timetable between 2020 and Mar 2024. One is in Shanshuijian phase 3. One is in the Xiaoyuanzi estate and has hosted 5-day Ashtanga workshops (FACT, previews) [S190].

### Free and cheap classes

- A studio listed a free Sunday class on four dates across 15 months, Feb 2023 to May 2024 (FACT) [S194].
- Five free or pay-what-you-wish yoga offers were found from 2019 to Nov 2026 (FACT) [S194].
- The cheapest paid Ashtanga found: 19.9 CNY for a first class, and 62.5 a class in a course of eight (FACT, 2021 to 2022; 500 / 8) [S189].

The step from a 20 CNY trial to 1,300 a month is steep. That is my reading.

### Closures

- One studio collapse is on record. An Old Town studio grew from about 20 to over 300 members on a year card of about 2,000 CNY. It fell apart in Sep 2020 (FACT) [S41].
- No report of a yoga or fitness closure in Dali was found for 2021 to 2026. One yoga guesthouse was offered for short let in Apr 2025 (FACT) [S201].
- Three Ashtanga rooms have tried Dali since 2020. One still shows a daily class in 2026 (FACT, from the notices above).

### Demand, estimated two ways

**Way 1, top-down** (start from all the people, then cut). ESTIMATE; kinds are in the last column [S248].

| Line | Low | Base | High | Kind |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Residents near | 95,442 | 95,442 | 162,036 | FACT (2020 census) + guess on who is "near" |
| Long-stayers near, on an average day | 7,089 | 14,178 | 24,812 | ESTIMATE x guess |
| Share who practise yoga steadily | 0.11% | 0.25% | 0.92% | VENDOR CLAIM (2021 national figure) [S203] x guess |
| Steady yoga people: residents | 102 | 237 | 1,494 | |
| Steady yoga people: long-stayers | 8 | 35 | 229 | |
| Share who would do a daily traditional practice | 1.0% | 2.0% | 5.0% | guess |
| Share who would pay about 1,300 a month: residents | 5.0% | 15.0% | 35.0% | guess, no source |
| Share who would pay about 1,300 a month: long-stayers | 5.0% | 10.0% | 30.0% | guess, no source |
| **Pool, whole town** | **0.1** | **0.8** | **29.6** | five unsure shares multiplied |

Reading: this chain cannot be tested. High is 542 times low. Its base of 0.8 is too low, because one room has stayed open for five years. It only shows how wide the guess is.

The national rate behind it: 3.0 to 3.5 million core studio users, plus about 3 million who practise steadily through gyms and online, in 1.41 billion people (VENDOR CLAIM, 2021, old) [S203]. In 2018, 65.8% of China's yoga users lived in its biggest cities (VENDOR CLAIM, old) [S202]. No survey gives a share for Ashtanga.

The same chain on other cities gives 7.44 month payers per 1 million urban residents (people in the built-up city). The sum is 0.00248 x 0.02 x 0.15 = 0.00000744 per resident. A city needs 2,605,241 residents to give the 19.4 a small room needs. Dali Town has 95,442 (ESTIMATE; residents are FACT, 2020 census, page re-opened on 23 Nov 2026) [S219][S2][S248].

| City | Urban residents, 2020 census | Month payers by the same chain |
|---|---|---|
| Guangzhou | 16,096,724 | 119.8 |
| Chengdu | 13,568,357 | 100.9 |
| Suzhou | 5,892,892 | 43.8 |
| Kunming | 5,273,144 | 39.2 |
| Hefei | 5,055,978 | 37.6 |
| Huizhou | 2,900,113 | 21.6 |
| Zhuhai | 2,207,090 | 16.4 |
| Maoming | 1,307,802 | 9.7 |
| Dali Town | 95,442 | 0.7 |

Every share in the chain is a guess, so read the order, not the numbers. Dali is last. Three private counts would rank the cities better: graduates with a running room, 2026 workshop heads, and seats sent to the In-Depth course by city. They are not counted yet. Carne and Neo can count them.

**Way 2, bottom-up** (count the rooms that exist). All guesses [S248].

| Line | Low | Base | High | Kind |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Month members, listed Mysore room | 3 | 5 | 10 | guess: class size is not published |
| Month members, lakeside courtyard | 0 | 1 | 3 | guess |
| Month members, third yard | 0 | 0 | 0 | guess |
| **Pool in the rooms that exist** | **3** | **6** | **13** | |
| Income this gives the listed room at 1,100 CNY a month | 3,300 | 5,500 | 11,000 | ESTIMATE, CNY a month |

Reading: the bottom-up count is the only anchor. Those people are already in a room.

**Where a new room's members would come from** (all guesses) [S248]

| Case | Taken from rooms that exist | Newly made month payers | Places held | Paying on average | Taken from the listed teacher | That teacher's loss a month, CNY |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| low | 0.1 x 3 = 0.3 | 0.0 | **0.3** | 0.26 | 0.3 of 3 | 330 of 3,300 (10.0%) |
| base | 0.2 x 6 = 1.2 | 1.0 | **2.2** | 1.93 | 1.0 of 5 | 1,100 of 5,500 (20.0%) |
| high | 0.4 x 13 = 5.2 | 2.0 | **7.2** | 6.30 | 4.0 of 10 | 4,400 of 11,000 (40.0%) |

Settled members leave at 50% a year and a place stays empty 3 months (guesses). So paying members are 87.5% of places held. For scale, gyms worldwide lose 33.6% of their members a year (ESTIMATE: 100% - 66.4% kept, a FACT for 2024; gyms, not yoga, not China) [S215].

**The three cases the model uses** (all guesses) [S248]

| Case | Settled members, paying | Long-stayers on a pass, yearly average | Month members, yearly average | Week cards in a middle month | Drop-ins in a middle month | Workshop |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| low | 0.26 | 0.00 | **0.3** | 0.5 | 1 | 1 a year, 5 students at 3,500 CNY |
| base | 1.93 | 0.44 | **2.4** | 1 | 3 | 1 a year, 7 students at 3,680 CNY |
| high | 6.30 | 1.76 | **8.1** | 4 | 10 | 2 a year, 16 students would pay 4,280 CNY |

The long-stayer line fails its own test. The base case needs 4 long-stay sign-ups a year. The top-down chain gives 0.3 for the whole town. So that line rests on the guess alone.

One count would replace most of this: the mats in use in the listed room on a few mornings. Yesterday's plan asks for it by Sun 29 Nov 2026.

## 3. Which area

**No area is proven.** One gate sits above the whole table: the teacher of the existing Mysore room has not been asked. Every area on the Old Town side draws on that teacher's small base.

How the table was made. Each area got a score of 1 to 5 on eight things. People count four times. Reach at 6 am, rules, lease risk and distance from the existing Ashtanga classes count twice each. Rent, rooms for visitors, and heat and noise count once. The top score is 75. The scores are judgements, so treat them as guesses. Three hard gates (tests an area must pass): at least 3 on people, at least 2 on rules, and a space that can hold a business licence at a known asking rent. Rents are asking prices (FACT, adverts). Rents per m2 are ESTIMATES (rent / size) [S248].

| Rank | Area | Score of 75 | Asking rent per m2 a month, CNY | Who is near | Rules and space | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | C. Old Town inside the walls (Renmin Road, Boai Road, Hongwu Road, back streets) | 39 | 33 to 264 (four adverts, Jul to Sep 2026). Renmin Road 182 to 264. Boai Road 83. The Boai gate building 33: about 200 m2 on 4 floors at 80,000 a year, which is 6,667 a month. | Tourists first. Shopkeepers. Some long-stayers. 95,442 residents in Dali Town (2020). | Space found: shop units. Heritage rules (rules that protect old buildings) on fronts and outdoor adverts. Street clean-up drives in 2024 and Aug 2026. Transfer fees of 90,000 to 580,000 asked on main streets. No bus found before 6:30 (a festival timetable). No large single room shown. | First by the gates, lowest by score. Dear and rule-bound. |
| 2 | A2. Foothill belt beside the Old Town: Shanshuijian (山水间) gates, Hongsheng Road, Gantong | 50 | 27 to 48 for whole spaces of 180 to 270 m2 (Apr to Oct 2026). The model uses the cheapest: 180 m2 at 5,500 a month, 30.6 per m2. | The densest cluster found: the existing Mysore room, 2 more yoga studios, 4 gyms, a dance studio. Settled newcomers. | Space not proven: the cheap listings are homes. Neighbours' consent is needed. A home is let year by year. The fire step cannot be done in a dwelling. | Highest score. It is the existing room's own belt. |
| 3 | E. Villages at the Old Town's west and north edge: Congyuan, Dazhifang, Sanwenbi, Shang and Xia Jiyi | 48 | No rent per m2. Rooms 500 to 2,000 a month (Feb 2024). One 9-room yard asks 238,000 a year, two years up front. | Digital nomads and young long-stayers. A shared house and co-working sites. An Iyengar centre. | Space not proven. A village house needs a legal change of use. A takeover needs the owner's written consent. | Right people, space not proven. |

| 14 | H. Wanqiao | 37 | No rent per m2. A 12-room courtyard asks 50,000 a year. | 21,130 residents (2020). | A village house. By car only. | Fails the people gate. |
| 15 | M. Haidong and Lixiangbang, east shore | 36 | 140 (one square shop, weak source) | 32,411 residents (2020). Resort visitors. | Across the lake from where long-stayers live. | Fails the people gate. |
| 16 | N. Shuanglang and Wase, north-east shore | 28 | 556 (one pier shop, weak source) | 17,899 and 16,431 residents (2020). Day tourists. | Heritage town plus lake zone. | Fails the people and rules gates. |

Sources for the table: rents [S159] to [S173], with the two weak ones from [S172]; who is near [S175][S176][S177][S178][S15]; rules [S179][S180][S181][S33][S36][S183][S184]; residents [S2]; bus [S22][S174]. Kinds: residents, adverts, law texts and press counts are FACT. The area guide [S178] and the two weak rents [S172] are VENDOR CLAIMS. The scores are guesses.

**The top pick and the runner-up**

- By the gates, the only area that passes is **C, the Old Town inside the walls**. It has some of the right people and a unit that can hold a licence at a known asking rent. It also has the lowest score of the six areas with the right people.
- By score, the best is **A2, the foothill belt at the Shanshuijian gates**: 50 of 75. If the teacher says yes and the distance column is left out, it is still first with 46. Every cheap real listing is there. They are homes, and it is that teacher's own belt.
- The first model picked B. It falls to 46 and sixth place. Its only priced space is a villa at 13,000 CNY a month. It has its own Ashtanga classes. The home-as-business rule applies there too.
- Xiaguan scores 52. It has the shop units and not the people.

**What to see on the ground, before a score matters.** Each one is a message or a visit, so each needs your go.

1. One written rent and the property certificate (the paper that shows who owns the unit) for one shop unit. There are three places to ask. One is the lane 大理的小巷子 in Xiaoyuanzi phase 5. One is the Bedsheet Factory operator. One is the 270 m2 space at the Shanshuijian east gate, which asks 7,300 CNY a month all-in in an advert four months old [S163].
2. The Old Town building at the Boai gate: its transfer fee and its floor plan. The advert calls the fee "very low" and gives no amount and no plan [S164].
3. The market office: does Dali register a licence at a home address, and on what consent? One seller writes that a house with no yard cannot get papers to trade [S171].
4. The fire office: does a paid yoga room of about 50 m2 need the fire step before opening?
5. The official lake-zone map for the exact plot.
6. One timed trip at dawn. The first bus found leaves at 6:30, in a festival timetable [S22].
7. Heating at 6 am in January, and noise at night.

## 4. What size

How many members one mat serves (ESTIMATE; every input is a guess) [S248]: a morning window of 2.5 hours / 1.5 hours on the mat = 1.67 turns. A member comes 5 days of 6, so divide by 0.83. Sell only 80% of the turns. That gives 1.61 members per mat. The range is 1.29 to 2.41.

Space per mat is 2.5 to 3 m2 (VENDOR CLAIM, two studio-software firms) [S208][S209]. The practice room is 57% of the whole floor (FACT, one shala in Madrid: 170 of 300 m2) [S210]. One US teacher wrote that a Mysore morning brings about 25 students in three hours (VENDOR CLAIM, 2013, old) [S211]. Another wrote that a new programme may start with five people (VENDOR CLAIM, 2011, old) [S212].

**The three sizes asked for, and one smaller.** All figures are ESTIMATES from the model, for a room in a rented house, alone, with no workshop money [S248].

| Size | Mats (range) | Practice floor, m2 (range) | Total m2 | Month members it can serve (range) | Times the base-case demand | Set-up cost, CNY (all guesses low to all high) | Cash needed to open, CNY | Result a year at base, CNY | Break-even month members |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| micro (below the three asked for) | 6 (5 to 8) | 18 (12 to 24) | 32 | 9 (6 to 19) | 3.8 | 48,963 (25,489 to 90,768) | 173,280 | -163,652 | 17.9 |
| small | 10 (8 to 12) | 30 (20 to 36) | 53 | 16 (10 to 28) | 6.8 | 69,105 (37,816 to 127,947) | 196,351 | -180,423 | 19.4 |
| medium | 20 (16 to 24) | 60 (40 to 72) | 105 | 32 (20 to 57) | 13.5 | 119,461 (68,632 to 220,895) | 262,344 | -238,981 | 24.6 |
| large | 36 (30 to 40) | 108 (75 to 120) | 189 | 57 (38 to 96) | 24.1 | 204,279 (119,637 to 376,411) | 389,041 | -345,226 | 34.2 |

Break-even is the member count where income meets cost, with the teacher paid 6,000 CNY a month.

Set-up cost of the small room: fit-out 42,105 + props 3,000 + heating kit 5,250 + papers 2,000 + opening week 3,000 + rent during the fit-out 8,250 + agent fee 5,500 = **69,105 CNY**. Fit-out is the work that turns a bare space into a room. It is priced at 800 CNY per m2 on the 30 / 0.57 = 52.63 m2 the room uses. The 800 is a guess. It sits between 500 in one undated template (VENDOR CLAIM) [S213] and 1,500 to 2,500 in founders' claims from 2016 to 2017 (VENDOR CLAIM, old) [S214].

The space each size would have to rent (FACT, asking rents from adverts) [S160][S162]. Micro, small and medium all need the same 180 m2 ground-floor home at 5,500 CNY a month (27,863 THB). Nothing smaller on a ground floor was found. Large needs a 257 m2 home at 7,200. No advert shows one open room big enough for medium or large.

**The pick.** None of the three sizes pays in any demand case. Small loses least: -180,423 CNY a year at base, against -238,981 for medium and -345,226 for large. Small needs 19.4 month members and can serve 16. Base demand is 2.4.

If a room runs at all, the size is smaller than small: **6 mats** (5 to 8), 18 m2 of practice floor, 32 m2 in all, serving 9 month members. The high case is 8.0. It works only as a room in the teacher's own home, with no rent charged to it. Cash to open such a room: 13,924 CNY (13,924 x 5.066 = 70,539 THB).

Why so small: a medium room serves 13.5 times the base-case members. Sold full, with 32 members, it would need 5.3 times the whole town's guessed pool.

## 5. What income

How the model works. The teacher is the owner, a Chinese citizen with an individual business (个体工商户, the simple one-person business form). The owner's pay of 6,000 CNY a month counts as a cost. The room is judged alone first: month members, week cards, drop-ins. A visiting teacher's workshop is a separate line, because it is your days and not the room's. All figures below are ESTIMATES from the model [S248].

Income less costs can differ from "keeps" by 1 CNY. That is rounding in the model.

A home room of 6 mats needs 1.0 month member to cover its costs. It needs 3.2 for the teacher to keep the lowest legal wage (2,120 CNY a month). It needs 7.4 for the teacher to keep 6,000 a month. It can serve 9. Base is 2.4.

This sum leaves out the teacher's own home rent and social insurance. A home room is thin in law: it needs the neighbours' consent, it cannot do the fire step, and no licence address is sure. A foreign-owned company cannot use it.

The groups overlap, so they do not add up line by line: the seven lines sum to -187,956. Together they rebuild the first model: -180,423 + 205,682 = 25,259, against its 21,838. The 3,421 between them is tax that a personal allowance of 60,000 CNY removes. That allowance comes from the cost reviewer's memory. It was not opened.

Three more changes came from the skeptics:

- **Area.** A new hard gate asks for a space that can hold a licence at a known rent. The distance gate became a welcome question. B fell from first to sixth.
- **Ownership.** Four cases became six. A partner case and a hall-only case were added. The licence case is marked blocked.
- **The plan.** The first plan said: teach a workshop in a graduate's small room. The revised plan says: no shala, keep yesterday's default.

Some points were not taken. The distance column stays as a score, because the brief asks for it. The month-long course stays out, because it copies the In-Depth format at 16.9% of one seat. Workshops are 1 a year, because the 2026 stops ran once per city [S136]. That last cut makes AYBKK's figures lower than the skeptics' own re-runs.

### Against one 180K seat and the yearly goal

One seat is 35,531 CNY. One full cohort of 12 seats is 426,372 CNY. The goal in the planning files is about 33 million THB net a year, or 6,514,015 CNY. The files mark the goal as a guess [S134].

- Time wall. 30 days in a cohort bring 12 seats, gross. 30 days of Dali trips at base are 30 / 7 = 4.29 trips. At 16,377 CNY a trip that is about 70,185 CNY (the model's unrounded figure), which is 1.98 seats.
- No case moves toward the goal. The best case that the law does not block is 0.25% of it, with your days.
- To the room's owner: -180,423 CNY a year at base is 5.1 seats of the course, every year.

## 6. Who owns it

The ask named three owners: you, a graduate, a partner. The first model had four cases, (a) to (d). The skeptics added two: the partner case (e) and a workshop in a hall with no second room (f). All money figures are ESTIMATES from the model. Workshop lines use an example 70/30 split from a public article [S216]. The real figure is gross x [Boonchu to fill: AYBKK share].

It still loses money at base. |

| (a) You own it through a foreign-owned company and hire a teacher | A company in China that you own rents the Old Town building at 6,667 CNY a month and hires a teacher at 8,000 a month. | yes for the company; your teaching needs a work permit | -263,809 / **-230,117** / -101,496 | -246,494 | 38 in year 1 (21 set-up + 10 managing + 7 teaching); 17 a year after | -6,056 | 283,221 (1,434,800 THB, 8.0 seats) | No, in every demand case. |

For scale, one cohort teaching day is 14,212 CNY before costs.

**Case by case, in short**

- **(a) Your own company.** The room alone costs a loss of 246,494 CNY a year at base (275,167 low, 154,049 high). The workshop you teach there adds 16,377. A hired teacher earns 96,000 a year. The same person as owner would take -115,597, so nobody would want to own it either. Getting out after the lease costs about 43,211 CNY (guess). A daily room taught by you would need about 264 teaching days a year in China. Visa-free entry cannot cover that.
- **(b) Licence.** For scale only: 7% of this room's sales, after Chinese tax, is 401 / 2,115 / 7,643 CNY a year. That is 0.01 / 0.06 / 0.22 seat. The 7% is a US franchise's published rate [S217]. AYBKK has no licence terms: [Boonchu to fill: licence fee]. If all 24 people of two full cohorts ran a room like this, the model gives 50,769 CNY (24 x 2,115 is 50,760; the gap is rounding). That is 1.43 seats, or 11.9% of one cohort. Four things must be true before this is worth a second look:
  1. The licensed rooms sell 7,173,510 CNY a year between them. Only then do licences equal one cohort (426,372 CNY).
  2. A company with 2 shops it runs itself, each open over 1 year. A lawyer says if Bangkok counts as one. If only mainland shops count, the way in is case (a) twice: 566,443 CNY in the model (2 x 283,221, with rounding), which is 15.9 seats, and 42 set-up days.
  3. Door data that shows the habit system keeps more members. Here yearly retention (the share of members who stay) must rise from 50.0% to 55.5% just for the owner to come out level on a 7% fee.
  4. The China build of the software is paid for: [Boonchu to fill: China build cost].
- **(c) Graduate's room, no tie.** At high demand 16 students would pay and a 10-mat room holds 10. That costs AYBKK 28,170 CNY a year against a hall. The host loses 2,750 CNY on the workshop at low demand and 272 at base, so it may not be held at all.
- **(d) No shala.** It costs nothing. It gives up nothing on days you do not teach, because every other case earns 0 or a loss there. One paper step applies in every case, this one too: nobody has checked that the name is protected in China.
- **(e) Partner studio.** The operator's result a year with a paid teacher is -74,896 / -78,455 / -82,014 CNY at base, when the studio keeps 30% / 40% / 50% of daily sales. Those shares are a published range used as an example [S216]. Any real split is private: [Boonchu to fill: partner share]. With no fixed pay for the teacher, at 40%: -23,590 / -6,455 / 50,388 (low / base / high). Real numbers for this case may exist. Was the first partner location signed, and what did it earn? Only you know.
- **(f) Hall, no second room.** The host needs 7.2 students to cover the cost. A hall of 16 is where the day starts to pay: 5,032 CNY a day. Hall hire is not counted: not found.

**Seats sent.** The one way a graduate's room could matter is by sending students to the In-Depth course. A room that sends 0.06 seat a year already equals the 7% licence example. One seat a year is 16.8 times that. I would judge a graduate's room by seats sent and workshop heads, not by a fee. The count of seats by source city is not made yet. Carne can count it.

> [!warning] Rules and papers. I am not a lawyer or a tax adviser. This is not legal or tax advice.
>
> **What the law says** (texts read on copies, 23 Nov 2026)
>
> - Business form. A foreigner may not be an individual business owner. So a room you own must be a company (FACT, the 2024 list of fields closed to foreign money, note 2) [S220][S221].
> - Yoga and fitness are not on that list of 29 items (FACT) [S220].
> - A company can have one owner. The Company Law sets no lowest capital. Promised capital is paid in within 5 years (FACT) [S222].
> - Work. A foreigner who works in China needs a work permit and a work-type residence permit. The fine is 5,000 to 20,000 CNY for the worker and 10,000 CNY a person for whoever hires. Being sent home brings a ban on entry for 1 to 5 years (FACT) [S120].
> - Visa-free entry for Thai passports is 30 days a stay and 90 days in any 180. It does not cover work (FACT, in force 1 Mar 2024) [S119].
> - A 2014 rule on short-term work lists "completing specific work for a Chinese cooperation partner" for under 90 days. Volunteers who are unpaid, or paid from abroad, are left out. This is a law firm's list. The rule's own text was not opened (VENDOR CLAIM, note of 11 Dec 2014, re-opened 23 Nov 2026) [S246].
> - Work permit, class B. The usual line is a degree, 2 years of related work and age up to 60. The guide lets offices relax these. Over 60, the employer adds a letter that covers health insurance (FACT, the Shenzhen copy of the national guide; no Dali guide was found) [S223][S224]. The permit is free. The residence permit costs 400 to 1,000 CNY (FACT, a Jinan guide) [S225].
> - A home used for business needs the consent of every affected owner in the building (FACT, Civil Code Art. 279) [S181].
> - Leases. The longest term is 20 years. Sub-letting (a tenant renting the space on to someone else) needs the owner's consent, or the owner may end the lease (FACT, Civil Code Art. 705 and 716) [S181].
> - Fire. A "public gathering place" must pass a fire safety step before it opens. Opening without it costs 30,000 to 300,000 CNY. A police rule lists "commercial fitness and leisure places". A yoga room is not named (FACT) [S227][S228]. A full fire design review starts only above 2,500 m2 (FACT) [S229].
> - Hygiene licence (a health permit for public places). It covers public places except parks, sports venues and transport. Yunnan's own list was not found (FACT) [S226].
> - The lake. Within 100 m of the water on the west and north shores, nothing may be built, rebuilt or extended unless it serves the lake. Fines run from 200,000 to 1,000,000 CNY. The next zone allows only public works (FACT, 2019 text; a later change could not be ruled out) [S179].
> - The Old Town. No new building in the core. Outdoor adverts and outside fittings must fit the town's style. Street fronts of new or rebuilt buildings near the core use old-style materials. The law has no article on shop-sign size (FACT, 2022 text) [S180].
> - Guesthouses. A prefecture law has been in force since 1 Jan 2026. It matters for any live-in house (FACT) [S182][S32].
> - Yunnan's long-stay list bans lodging trade in rented houses without legal papers (FACT, in force 20 Nov 2026) [S35].
> - A licence does not save a business in a building without papers. In May 2026 one lake town told 7 licensed businesses in such buildings to stop (FACT, a press report opened by the cost reviewer) [S185].
> - Franchise. Only a company may be the franchisor. It needs 2 shops it runs itself, each open over 1 year. It files within 15 days of the first contract. Fines are 100,000 to 500,000 CNY (FACT) [S230]. Papers made abroad are accepted if they are legalised, which means stamped so that another country accepts them (FACT, filing measures, opened by the cost reviewer on a law-database copy) [S233]. In Aug 2026 the top court upheld a 100,000 CNY fine even after the fees were paid back. It also held a deal signed by a non-company void (FACT) [S231]. A law firm says fees under any name count, "trademark licence fee" included (VENDOR CLAIM) [S232].
> - The name. A trademark is a registered name or logo. The fee to file one is 270 CNY a class online. The office took 4 months on average in 2025. A new Trademark Law starts on 1 Jan 2027 (FACT) [S234][S235][S236]. The cost reviewer adds that China gives a trademark to the first to file. No law text was opened for that line.
> - Tax breaks to 31 Dec 2027. No VAT while sales stay under 100,000 CNY a month; a month that reaches the line is taxed in full, at 1%. Income tax of an individual business is halved. A small company pays 5% of profit (FACT) [S237][S238][S239].
> - Money sent abroad. A fee or a dividend (profit a company pays its owner) paid to a foreign company loses 10% at the source. The Chinese buyer also holds back 6% VAT on a licence fee (FACT) [S241][S242][S239].
> - Tax treaty (a deal between two countries on who taxes what). If your people give services in China for more than 183 days in any 12 months, China may tax the Thai side as if it had a base there (FACT, 1986 treaty, read on a copy) [S243].
> - Prepaid passes. A buyer may ask for the money back within 7 days. A buyer may cancel if an "unlimited" pass is not served as normal. The rule names fitness and training (FACT, top court rule in force 1 May 2025, Art. 1, 13 and 14, re-opened on a copy on 23 Nov 2026) [S108][S247]. Shanghai caps prepaid passes. Beijing holds back 30% of unused prepaid money at first. Neither is law in Yunnan (FACT) [S244][S245].
>
> **What is an estimate**
>
> - Licence fee. Of each 100 CNY paid to a Thai company, 84.91 arrive. Sum: 100 / 1.06 x 6% = 5.66 VAT; 10% of 94.34 = 9.43; 100 - 5.66 - 9.43 = 84.91.
> - Workshop tax at base. Your side at the example 70% is 18,032 CNY. Tax: 18,032 x 80% = 14,425.6; x 20% - 1,410 = 1,475.12. VAT at 1% = 180.32. Net 16,377.
> - Tax on 200,000 CNY of yearly profit. A Chinese owner's individual business: 14,750 CNY (30,000 x 5% + 60,000 x 10% + 110,000 x 20% = 29,500, halved) [S240][S238]. Your company, shares held by a Thai company: 10,000 + 19,000 = 29,000. Shares held by you as a person: 10,000 + 38,000 = 48,000. The 20% on dividends to a person is the reviewer's reading.
> - Papers. Home address 2,000 CNY, shop unit 8,000, foreign-owned company 40,000. All guesses. No Dali fee was found.
> - Social insurance. The 26% rate is a guess. The 4,357 floor comes from one self-media page.
> - Days. 2026 had 33 teaching days in China over 8 stops (FACT, yesterday's report) [S136]. With a travel day each side that is 33 + 2 x 8 = 49 days. One Dali trip adds 7: about 56 of the 90 visa-free days, if all fell in one window of 180 days.
>
> **What a Chinese lawyer or tax adviser must confirm**
>
> 1. Does a paid 5-day workshop at a host studio need short-term work papers, and which visa? Can a Thai company invoice the host? Who is the organiser on paper? This covers every China stop.
> 2. Does Dali's market office register a business licence at a home address, and on what consent of the neighbours?
> 3. Who holds title to a given estate plot, and which lake zone is it in?
> 4. Does a paid yoga room of about 50 m2 need the fire step before opening in Dali? Does it need a hygiene licence if there is no shower?
> 5. Does a deal with no name in it, or a split inside a partner's studio, stay outside the franchise rule? Can the In-Depth course fee be read as a franchise fee? Do shops outside China count toward the two?
> 6. Can an owner who teaches in their own company get a work permit, given the degree, work and age lines?
> 7. Pass and course terms under the prepaid rules. A lease clause on who owns the fit-out. The owner's written consent before any transfer fee is paid.
> 8. The VAT rate on a visiting teacher's fee: 1%, or 6 parts in 106. Tax on dividends to a foreign person. How money may leave China, after a host transfer was blocked in July 2026 [S139].
> 9. Health data of students in China on a server abroad, and a WeChat mini-program, for the habit system.

## 7. What could kill it, and what the student gets

**Munger's inversion, in one line:** ask "what would kill this?" first, then stay away from those things.

What would kill a Dali shala (numbers are ESTIMATES from the model [S248] unless tagged):

1. **No spare demand.** Base demand is 2.4 month members. A small rented room needs 19.4.
2. **The teacher already there.** At base, 1.0 of that teacher's guessed 5 month members moves over. That is 1,100 of a guessed 5,500 CNY a month, or 20%. Nobody has asked the teacher there.
3. **No lawful space where the people live.** Homes need the neighbours' consent. One neighbour's complaint about 6 am visitors could stop the room after the floor is paid for. That last line is a reading of the Civil Code.
4. **It closes in public.** The owner's take is -108,423 CNY a year at base. The lowest legal wage is 25,440 a year. The room would close after you taught in it, or with the name on it.
5. **A ban.** Paid teaching on visa-free entry is not settled. A ban ends every China stop. Its price is [Boonchu to fill: yearly net of all China stops] x 1 to 5 years.
6. **The franchise rule.** The lowest fine, 100,000 CNY, is 47.3 years of this room's 7% fee.
7. **One teacher.** No cover teacher is known in Dali. The existing room printed a 26-week term.
8. **Refunds.** A one-teacher room that shuts for weeks may owe money back on "unlimited" passes [S247].
9. **Money out of China.** A host transfer was blocked in July 2026 [S139].
10. **A hint of authorization.** The In-Depth course gives a certificate of completion only. No room may say or hint at more.
11. **The name.** Nobody has searched if AYBKK or Ashtanga Yoga Bangkok is filed in China.
12. **Bangkok.** Who runs the shala while you are away? It was asked on 1 Nov for the Mysore trip. No answer is in the files [S135].
13. **Attention.** Cohort 3 starts in 49 days.
14. **The "healing" label.** In Dali, yoga is sold with add-ons. One 2026 Ashtanga retreat class there comes with a singing-bowl certificate (FACT, preview) [S64]. That fights "from trend to habit" [S142].

**Hormozi's value equation, in one line:** an offer is worth more when the dream outcome is big and the buyer believes it will work, and worth less when the wait is long and the effort is high.

What a Dali student gets from a second daily room, set against the room that is there:

| Part | The room that is there | A new room run by a graduate | Better or worse |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dream outcome | A daily practice that lasts | The same | Same |
| Belief it will work | The teacher is on the Sharath Yoga Centre list and has taught in Dali since Oct 2021 [S59][S62] | A graduate who is not on that list. In China such listings are used as a buying checklist (AYBKK note) [S141]. | Worse |
| Time delay | A daily room, six days a week | The same | Same |
| Effort and price | 1,300 CNY a month, Mysore only [S61] | The same price at most. A room that is not on the list cannot charge more for the same class (guess). | Same or worse |

So a second room sells the same thing with less belief. I can see two gaps it could fill: a 6:30 start, and the second half of the year. Both are guesses. The teacher there moved the start from 6:30 to 9:00, which hints that the early hour did not fill. That is my reading.

What a student gets from one visiting workshop is better. A 5-day workshop costs 3,680 CNY in the model, or 2.8 months of the local room (ESTIMATE: 3,680 / 1,300). When it ends, there is a daily Mysore room in town to carry on in. That fits "a teacher who notices when you are gone". It also sends students to that room, not away from it.

The habit system is the one thing AYBKK could add that the room there does not have. No door data yet shows what it is worth in a room you do not see. The "see you tomorrow" list is built as a draft and ships switched off [S135].

## 8. Recommendation

**The plan in one sentence:** no shala in Dali; keep yesterday's default until 9 Feb 2027, and read the teacher's answer and the counts on Fri 11 Dec 2026.

**Stakes:** one more paid seat in cohort 3 is 35,531 CNY and no extra days. That equals 2.2 Dali trips (ESTIMATE: 35,531 / 16,377) [S248].

**Why care:** the hope is a studio that earns on days you do not teach. Here every case earns 0 or a loss on those days (ESTIMATE) [S248].

The map of five business levels (Neo's reading, which you have not agreed to): this would be a second level 1 room, the lowest level, and a much smaller one. It builds no money on days you do not teach. For "sticky", which means students who stay: daily practice in Dali already has a home.

Why not more of what already pays: that is the better use. Seven days in Dali bring 16,377 CNY at base, on the example split. One more seat in cohort 3 brings 35,531 CNY and takes no extra day.

### Steps

Today is Mon 23 Nov 2026. Cohort 3 runs 11 Jan to 9 Feb 2027. No step needs a trip or a payment before your go.

| # | Step | Owner | Day |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Answer the closing question below: park the Dali shala, yes or no. | Boonchu | Your pick. My suggestion: by Fri 27 Nov 2026. |
| 2 | Send yesterday's step 2(b) to the teacher of the Dali Mysore room: how many practise each morning, and is a visit welcome? I would not ask about a second room. | Boonchu sends; Carne drafts | by Sun 29 Nov 2026 |
| 3 | Count 2026 workshop heads by stop, and Yunnan cities on the forms. Counts only, no names. | Neo | by Sun 29 Nov 2026 |
| 4 | Count graduates with a running room, by city, and seats in cohorts 1 to 3 by source city. Counts only, kept private. | Carne | by Fri 4 Dec 2026 |
| 5 | If the teacher replies, put that number into the model in place of 3 / 5 / 10 and run it again. | Neo | within a day of the reply; latest Thu 10 Dec 2026 |
| 6 | The lawyer hour from yesterday's step 4. Add the nine questions from the box in section 6. | Boonchu | Your pick. My suggestion: before Fri 11 Dec 2026. |
| 7 | Say yes or no to a trademark search and filing in China, class 41 (the class for teaching): 270 CNY official fee online, agent fee not found. | Boonchu | by Fri 11 Dec 2026 |
| 8 | Read once: the teacher's answer, the counts, the lawyer's answers. Then park Dali. | Boonchu, with Machi | Fri 11 Dec 2026 |
| 9 | Nothing on Dali. Cohort 3. | all | 12 Dec 2026 to 9 Feb 2027 |

Dates. No trip dates are set here. So the family school calendar and the two 90/180 day counts were not run on dates. The day sum alone: about 56 of 90 days, if one Dali trip falls in the same 180 days as a 2026-size tour. Steps 1 to 8 are messages, counts and one hour with a lawyer. None needs a trip, so the school breaks do not matter here.

### The cheapest test before any lease is signed

A lease is the last step, not the first. Each stage must pass before the next one starts. Stages 2 to 4 wait until after 9 Feb 2027 and need your go.

| Stage | What | Cash | Pass rule | Stop rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The teacher's answer: the morning head count and the welcome. | 0 | The teacher gives a count and says a visit is welcome. | Fewer than 20 month members in that room. A small rented room needs 19.4, so it could only fill by taking all of them. |
| 2 | One written rent and the property certificate for one shop unit. The lawyer's answers on the licence address and the fire step. | Lawyer's fee: [Boonchu to fill: lawyer's fee]. Rent quotes: 0. | A unit that can hold a licence, at a rent that brings break-even under the heads counted in stage 1. | No such unit. |
| 3 | Twelve weeks of morning hours hired in a studio that is already there. Month passes only. No AYBKK name on the door. Count paying month members on day 1 and at week 12, the same way. | Hourly hire: not found; a Dali studio can quote it. All the income is 54 CNY per open hour at base, so the hour must cost well under that. | Paying month members at week 12 reach the break-even of the unit found in stage 2. | Fewer than 3.2 paying month members at week 12. That is the count at which a home room pays the lowest legal wage. |
| 4 | Only then a lease. | 196,351 CNY for a small room in the cheapest house found | | |

If a graduate in Dali wants to teach at home on their own, that is their choice and their home. I would keep AYBKK's name, money and software out of it, because the form is thin in law.

### What we know, think, and may be wrong about

| We know (FACT) | We think (ESTIMATE) | We may be wrong about |
|---|---|---|
| One daily Mysore room has run in Dali since Oct 2021. Its month price has not risen since 2023. Its week and single prices were cut [S61][S62]. | Base demand for a new room is 2.4 month members (0.3 to 8.1). | The head count in the room that is there. It is guessed at 5 (3 to 10). A count of 20 or more would change the demand table. |
| The cheapest real space that is big enough is a home: 180 m2 at 5,500 CNY a month, an asking rent [S160]. | A small rented room loses 180,423 CNY a year at base and needs 19.4 members. | A small shop unit at a low rent may exist near the Shanshuijian gates. None was found online. |
| A franchise needs 2 shops run by the brand owner, each open over a year [S230]. A foreigner cannot hold an individual business [S220]. | A licence would bring 2,115 CNY a year from a room like this, if it were allowed. | Shops outside China may count. A deal with no name in it may fall outside the rule. Only a lawyer can say. |
| Visa-free entry does not cover work [S119][S120]. | One Dali workshop nets AYBKK 16,377 CNY for 7 days away, on an example split. | A short paid workshop may not count as work. The real split and the host's real cost are in your ledgers. |
| The local economy is weak in 2026, and long-stay numbers are up 24.89% [S145][S7]. | Long-stayers add 0.44 month member to a new room at base. | Long-stayers may pay more than the chain says. The line has no evidence either way. |
| No paid wage for a yoga teacher in Dali was found. | A teacher draws 6,000 CNY a month. It is the biggest single swing: 60,000 CNY a year. | A teacher who already lives in Dali on other income may need less. |

### Questions only Boonchu can answer

1. Why Dali: a graduate's invite, a retreat idea, or a place you and the family want to be? This was asked on 22 Nov.
2. Who did you have in mind as owner and teacher? Whose cash, and what is the most you would put at risk?
3. Does any graduate of cohort 1 or 2 live in Dali or Yunnan, with a room and a business licence?
4. Do you know the teacher of the Dali Mysore room? Would a visit be welcome?
5. What does a normal China stop net per day away? [Boonchu to fill: net per workshop day], [Boonchu to fill: host cost per stop], [Boonchu to fill: yearly net of all China stops].
6. Bangkok's own numbers, as the real base for sizing any second room: [Boonchu to fill: Bangkok members], [Boonchu to fill: Bangkok monthly cost], [Boonchu to fill: Bangkok net], [Boonchu to fill: Bangkok class capacity].
7. Was the first partner location signed, and is it running? Is that deal the form you have in mind for Dali?
8. Do you wish to license the name or the habit system to graduates at all? Nothing is written on it.
9. Which entry status do you use to teach in China? Has a Chinese lawyer confirmed it in writing?
10. Is about 33 million THB net a year still the goal? Do you agree with Neo's reading that money on days you do not teach is the missing piece?
11. How many of the 12 seats in cohort 3 are paid today?

If Dali is a place you and the family want to be, say so. That is a different sum from this one.

**Do you agree to park the Dali shala with the Dali trip until 9 Feb 2027, yes or no?**

## 2026-11-22 · carnegie-dali-yoga-fitness-ashtanga

---
title: Dali Yoga Fitness Ashtanga Research
type: research
status: draft
tags: [aybkk, research, china, dali, yunnan, workshops]

---

# Yoga, fitness and Ashtanga in Dali (Tali), China

Research report for Boonchu Tanti, AYBKK (Ashtanga Yoga Bangkok). Written on 22 Nov 2026. "Tali" was read as Dali (大理), in Yunnan province, China. The tags used below (FACT, VENDOR CLAIM, ESTIMATE, [S12]) are explained right after the summary.

## 0. One-page summary

**Answer: do not teach in Dali in 2027 unless a host brings a room, papers and paid deposits, because the serious Ashtanga base there is small: one listed Mysore room and visiting groups of 12 to 25.**

Dali is a real long-stay and "healing" town. Its serious Ashtanga base is a different and much smaller group. A deposit is money paid up front to hold a place. "Papers" means a business licence (the paper that lets a firm trade). "Listed" means on the Sharath Yoga Centre teacher list.

**One action:** run the 19-day paper test (three counts, two messages, one post) and read it once on Fri 11 Dec 2026. No trip, no money.

**Stakes:** cohort 3 (the next 30-day group) starts in 50 days with 0 new leads (people who asked about the course) on 21 Nov; an empty seat costs 180,000 THB, and a whole 5-day Dali workshop would take in only about 1.4 to 2.9 seats before the host's share and tax (ESTIMATE) [S135][S140][S133].

**Why care:** the belief is "Dali is full of wellness people, so it is a market", but a community founder there says healing brings mostly passers-by, the budgets found are small, and the real wall is teaching days, not towns [S8][S17][S134].

**The 5 numbers that matter**

| # | Number | What it is | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Nearly 200,000 | Long-stay people in Dali City. Official counts do not agree with each other. | FACT, first half of 2026 | [S8] |
| 2 | 77.26 days | Average long stay, Dali prefecture | FACT, Jan to Sep 2026 | [S7] |
| 3 | 1 of 45 | Teachers in Dali on the Sharath Yoga Centre list for China (Hong Kong included) | FACT, read 22 Nov 2026 | [S59] |
| 4 | 49,200 to 102,500 CNY (249,000 to 519,000 THB) | Gross (money in, before any cost) of a 5-day Dali workshop: 12 to 25 students x 4,100 CNY. One 180K seat is about 35,500 CNY. | ESTIMATE | [S67][S65][S139][S133] |
| 5 | 5,000 to 20,000 CNY (25,000 to 101,000 THB), and a ban on entry for 1 to 5 years | Fine for a foreigner who works in China without a work permit. The ban follows if he is sent home. | FACT, law in force since 2013 | [S120][S133] |

**The 3 biggest risks**

1. Work rules. Visa-free entry (coming in without a visa) does not cover work. Whether a short paid workshop counts as work is not settled. A ban would end every China stop, not only Dali [S119][S120].
2. Small base, small budgets. One listed room with unknown class size. Visiting events of 12 to 25 people. Long-stayers budget about 2,200 to 7,300 CNY a month (rough blog figures) [S59][S67][S17].
3. Distraction. Dali planning takes days away from filling cohort 3. The best Dali case only matches one cohort teaching day, before the host's share and tax (ESTIMATE, section 7).

**The cheapest test**

Three counts, two messages, one post. Cost 0 THB. About 30 minutes of Boonchu's time. Read once on Fri 11 Dec 2026. The steps and the pass and kill rules are in section 8. The test cannot say whether a paid teaching visit is legal. That needs a lawyer.

## How to read this note

- "Tali" was read as Dali (大理), in Yunnan province. Dali Town was once spelled Tali [S23].
- Two places share the name. **Dali City** (大理市) is the small city that holds Xiaguan (the modern centre), the Old Town and the lake villages. **Dali prefecture** (大理州) is the big area around it: Dali City plus 11 counties. A prefecture is one level below a province. Each number says which place it is for.
- Each figure has a tag. **FACT**: an official page, a law text, the press or a published price says it. **VENDOR CLAIM**: a business or a paid-report teaser says it. **ESTIMATE**: my own sum, with the sum shown. **Not confirmed**: the claim could not be checked at its own source.
- [S12] means source 12 in the list at the end.
- Prices are in CNY (Chinese yuan, also called RMB). A few key numbers also show Thai baht in brackets at 1 CNY = 5.066 THB, the rate fetched on 22 Nov 2026 [S133]. Prices printed in USD, SGD or EUR stay as printed.
- Limits. The checkers re-opened the source pages on 22 Nov 2026. They had no web searches left to hunt for second sources. WeChat prices were read in public search previews, not in the full posts. Open the source before any number goes public.

## 1. Dali in short

### Who lives there

| What | Dali City (大理市) | Dali prefecture (大理州) | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Residents | 771,128 | 3.321 million | FACT, 2020 census (city), end 2025 (prefecture) | [S1][S3] |
| Urban income per person, per year | 51,005 CNY | 46,824 CNY | FACT, 2025 | [S5][S3] |
| Urban spending per person, per year | not found | 34,344 CNY | FACT, 2025 | [S3] |
| The same, per month | not found | about 2,860 CNY | ESTIMATE: 34,344 / 12 | [S3] |
| Births and deaths in the year | not found | 18,200 births, 27,100 deaths | FACT, 2025 | [S3] |

Inside Dali City, about 395,000 people live in the three Xiaguan-side districts. Dali Town, which holds the Old Town, has 95,442 (FACT, 2020 census; the 395,000 is a sum of three districts) [S2]. The Old Town is 10 km north of Xiaguan (FACT) [S23].

The local base is shrinking and local wallets are modest. So buyers of higher-priced classes would be long-stayers and visitors. That last line is a reading, not a published fact.

### Long-stay people (旅居)

"Long-stay" means people from other places who live in Dali for weeks or months. No source says how many nights count. The official counts do not match:

| Count | Place | Period | Kind | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 449,100 (up 44.96%) | printed as "Dali", read as the prefecture | 2025 | FACT | [S9] |
| 407,000 (up 49.4%) | prefecture | 2025 | FACT | [S10] |
| Over 600,000 | prefecture | Jan to Jul 2025 | FACT | [S11] |
| 286,100 (up 51.94%) | prefecture | Jan to Oct 2025 | FACT | [S12] |
| Nearly 200,000 | Dali City | first half of 2026 | FACT | [S8] |
| 333,700 (up 24.89%) | prefecture | Jan to Sep 2026 | FACT | [S7] |
| About 250,500 | Dali City | Jan to Sep 2026 | ESTIMATE: 333,700 x 75.07% city share | [S7] |

Use any of these only with its date and its source. The 2026 target for the prefecture is growth of 10% or more (FACT) [S10]. Yunnan province counted over 5.5 million long-stay people in 2025, who stayed 83 days on average (FACT) [S13].

Who they are. No Dali-only profile was found. For Yunnan as a whole, about 60% are aged 20 to 40 (FACT, survey cited in Aug 2025) [S14]. One co-living house (a shared house for long-stayers) in Dali, NCC, described its people. One third hold a postgraduate degree. 70% come from first-tier cities (China's biggest cities). Nearly half have steady income from remote or freelance work (FACT, one house, Feb 2024) [S15]. The founder of a long-stay community in Dali City said wellness and healing "bring mostly passers-by" (FACT, Sep 2026) [S8].

### Who visits, and when

| What | Figure | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Visits, prefecture | 121.49 million in 2025; 110.04 million in 2024 | FACT | [S3][S4] |
| Tourism spending, prefecture | 139.355 billion CNY in 2025; 171.66 billion in 2024 | FACT | [S3][S4] |
| Spending per visit, prefecture | about 1,147 CNY in 2025; about 1,560 in 2024 | ESTIMATE: spending / visits | [S3][S4] |
| Visits and spending, Dali City | 78.08 million; 94.67 billion CNY | FACT, 2025 | [S6] |
| Foreign visits, prefecture | 685,900 in 2025; 453,000 in 2024 | FACT | [S3][S4] |

Visits count trips, not people. More trips came in 2025, and each trip brought less money. No source says why.

Peak season is about 80 days a year (FACT, 2025 trade article) [S21]: Chinese New Year, May Day with the March Street festival, the summer school holiday and National Day week. March Street ran from 1 to 7 May in 2026 (FACT) [S22]. For 2027 it should fall around 21 to 27 April (ESTIMATE from the lunar calendar; not yet announced).

Weather (FACT, normals for 1991 to 2020, Dali City) [S23]:

- Altitude: about 1,975 m.
- Rain: June to October, 92 to 220 mm a month.
- Dry and sunny: November to April, 11 to 34 mm a month.
- Cold mornings: the January average low is 3.1 C.

### How to get there

- No direct flight from Bangkok shows now. West Air began Dali to Bangkok on 25 Feb 2025, three a week (FACT) [S25]. In Nov 2026, Trip.com shows only trips with stops, and the airport's winter notice names only Beijing, Guangzhou and Shanghai, 14 flights a day (FACT, read 22 to 23 Nov 2026) [S26][S27]. This is a strong sign, not proof. Check a live booking.
- Bangkok to Kunming: nonstop, about 2 h 21 min, 48 to 63 flights a week, return from US$163 (FACT, Trip.com low fares, Nov 2026) [S28].
- Kunming to Dali by fast train: 103 trains listed for 23 Nov 2026, fastest 1 h 55 min, second class 105 to 155.5 CNY (FACT) [S29].
- Dali station is in Xiaguan. The Old Town is 45 to 60 minutes away by bus (FACT, 2019 data, old) [S30].
- Dali airport is about 12 km from Xiaguan. It handled 3.75 million passengers in 2025 (FACT) [S31][S9].
- Door to door from Bangkok: a full day each way (ESTIMATE: flight, station change, train, transfer).

### What it costs

| Item | Price (CNY) | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Room in a village near the Old Town | 500 a month | FACT, Feb 2024 | [S15] |
| Co-living room (NCC) | 1,000 to 2,000 a month | FACT, Feb 2024 | [S15] |
| Two-bedroom flat, Xiaguan side | 1,000 to 1,600 a month (four listings; these are the prices owners ask) | FACT, Nov 2026 | [S16] |
| Monthly living budget | about 2,200 (thrifty), 4,100 (comfortable), 7,300 (high) | ESTIMATE (a blog compilation's rough figures, not my sum), low confidence | [S17] |
| Guesthouse near the Old Town | 100 to 300 a day | FACT, Jul 2023 | [S18] |
| Guesthouse, normal weekday | 200 or more a night; promotional beds from 50 | FACT, Jul 2026 | [S19][S20] |
| Summer price rise near the Old Town | 30% to 50% | FACT (a commentator's summary), Jul 2026 | [S20] |
| Guesthouse, peak season | 250 to 500 a night, about double the low season | FACT, 2025 | [S21] |

Rooms are plentiful. The prefecture had 7,007 working guesthouses (FACT; end of July 2025 in one source, end of 2025 in another) [S32][S19]. Operators said fewer than half of their rooms were full in summer 2026 (FACT, operators' accounts) [S19].

## 2. Yoga and the healing scene

### The picture

- The New York Times wrote in Feb 2024 that "healing" is the buzzword in Dali: "Healing yoga, healing camping trips, even healing coffee shops" (FACT) [S24].
- A business-registry count gave 61 yoga-related studios and shops in "Dali" (FACT as cited by the press, Mar 2023; city or prefecture not stated) [S38]. For scale, Meituan listed over 300 yoga studios in Kunming (FACT, May 2023, a different method) [S39]. No newer count was found.
- The healing trade sells short things. Healer trainings last 3 to 5 days, or 1 to 2 weeks for advanced levels (FACT, a journalist's summary, Mar 2023) [S38]. Drama-healing stays start at about 1,000 CNY per person per day (FACT, drama healing only, Mar 2023) [S38]. There is no sourced Dali-wide price per day for healing stays.
- The Chinese press treats this trade with doubt. One report quotes a 5-day course in Dali at 6,980 CNY, with a 3,980 CNY add-on to become a "promotion ambassador" (VENDOR CLAIM, Apr 2024) [S45].
- The government's wellness plans are about medicine, tourism and outdoor sport, not yoga. The prefecture was named a "World Wellness City" pilot (FACT; the body that gave the title is not confirmed) [S10][S55]. In the two prefecture papers read, the 2026 work report and the outdoor sport plan, the word yoga appears once, as "forest yoga" (FACT, Aug 2025) [S10][S86]. A long official review of Dali never uses the words yoga or healing (FACT, Dec 2025) [S12]. Xinhua says sport and medical long-stay products are in short supply (FACT, Nov 2026) [S7].
- Trust has been broken before. An Old Town studio with over 300 members closed in Sep 2020 when the owner vanished, and Dali City police opened a case (FACT) [S41]. One refund complaint about a 6,400 CNY yoga card is on public record (FACT, one buyer's account, Aug 2024) [S91].

## 3. Ashtanga in Dali, Yunnan and China

A note on the WeChat prices in this section. They were read in search previews. Where a year is not printed in the post title, the year is the first researcher's reading.

### Dali: what exists

- One listed teacher. The Sharath Yoga Centre list shows 45 teachers under China, Hong Kong included. One is in Dali: Level 2, shala name OSAYS (FACT, read 22 Nov 2026; the list shows no update date) [S59]. The address is the same as Dawn Yoga, so it is one room with two names. The list proves a listing and an address. It does not prove the room is open today.
- Its own notice says the room opened in Oct 2021 (FACT) [S62]. An earlier notice charged 1,530 a month, 900 a week and 200 a class. So the 2026 prices are a little lower, not higher (FACT) [S62][S61].
- A second place, the Dali Ashtanga Yoga courtyard, has sold Mysore classes on a week card, 100-hour courses and retreat classes. Its teacher is not on the Sharath Yoga Centre list (FACT) [S59][S63].
- Visiting groups have come many times. At least seven organisers have sold Ashtanga weeks or courses in Dali (ESTIMATE: my count across [S63] to [S70] and [S85]).

| Organiser | Format in Dali | Price (CNY) | Group | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Tree Yoga (Guangzhou) | 6 days in the Old Town, lodging included, with a gong bath | 8,999 list; 7,999 early | 12 places | FACT, year read as 2023 | [S67] |
| Yan (Chengdu, Level 2) with LZ Ashtanga (Kunming) | 5 days with room and food | 8,800 list; 7,900 early | not shown | FACT, year read as 2023 | [S66] |
| Yang Kun Yoga (Xi'an) | 7 days, Dali and Lijiang | 4,880; 4,280 early; 3,980 for past students | not confirmed | FACT, year read as 2026 | [S68] |
| Yogaseed | 5 days, tuition only, sold as yoga and not only Ashtanga | 6,500 to 6,700 | not shown | FACT, 2026 | [S69] |
| Yin Chaowen with TheYogaBook | 5 days | 4,680; 4,280 early | not shown | FACT, years read as 2021 to 2024 | [S70] |
| Courtyard (Alan) | 100-hour course | 12,880 list; 7,580 early | cap of 20 | FACT, Nov 2023 | [S65] |
| Courtyard (Alan) | Retreat class with a singing-bowl certificate | 3,680 | not shown | FACT, 2026 | [S64] |

"List" is the full price. "Early" is the early-bird price: cheaper for those who pay early. "Cap" is the upper limit on places.

Per day, the weeks and short courses come to about 570 to 1,760 CNY (ESTIMATE: price / days; 3,980 / 7 = 569 and 8,800 / 5 = 1,760) [S68][S66]. General Dali retreats that are not Ashtanga mostly cost 2,680 to 3,680 CNY for 3 to 5 days (ESTIMATE from seven preview prices, low confidence) [S67].

### Dali: what does not exist, as far as was found

- A second listed teacher in Dali.
- Any Ashtanga event in Dali from 2023 to 2026 led by a visiting foreign teacher.
- A class size for the Dali Mysore room.
- Proof that the courtyard's daily class still runs in 2026.

### Yunnan

- Kunming. LZ Ashtanga ran a one-month study course for 6 students (FACT, 2023; price not found) [S71]. It hosted a Level 2 visitor, Ajay Tokas, for 5 days in June 2024 at 4,600 CNY list, 3,680 if paid a month early (FACT) [S72]. Other Kunming Ashtanga names show up in WeChat: Ashtanga瑜堂, ALin瑜伽, SpaceYogaShala, GossipCup (FACT, names only) [S73][S70]. One list entry reads "Sanya & Kunming" (FACT) [S59].
- Mile. A Level 2 teacher based in South Africa, Ethan Ward, advertised a 5-day workshop there for 25 to 29 Apr 2026, with 15 places. Price not found (FACT) [S74]. So a listed teacher from abroad did sell a workshop in Yunnan in 2026, but not in Dali.
- Lijiang. No Mysore room was found, only visiting courses (FACT, one search page and the list) [S75][S59].
- Chinese teachers use Yunnan for 15-day courses. One advert charged 2,900 CNY for 15 days of room and food in Xishuangbanna (FACT, year not shown) [S85].

### China

Of the 45 listed teachers, 13 give Hong Kong addresses and 32 give mainland cities: Shanghai 12, Beijing 6, Guangzhou 3, Hangzhou 3, Wuhan 2, Chengdu 2, and one each for Tianjin, Wuhu, Dali and "Sanya & Kunming". By level: 30 Level 2, 14 Level 1, 1 Certified (FACT for the 45; the splits are the checker's count) [S59]. So Level 2 is common in China. On the same list: Republic of Korea 68, Japan 52, India 44 (FACT) [S60].

### Benchmark prices: workshops and long programs

All prices in this table are FACT (published prices, 2022 to 2026). What the table shows:

- A visiting-teacher workshop costs about 470 to 1,380 CNY per teaching day (ESTIMATE: 2,800 / 6 = 467 and 6,880 / 5 = 1,376) [S82][S78].
- Per day, the 2024 Xi'an price looks low. Per hour it does not: about 204 CNY, against 140 to 193 for the Darby courses. The Xi'an days were 3 hours long.
- Teachers charge about the same in Yunnan as in other Chinese cities. The place does not set the price [S70][S72].
- Mysore room by the month: Dali 1,500 CNY (FACT, 2026) [S61]. Another Chinese room listed 880 a month in 2023, and Tree Yoga advertised Mysore at 999 for a new branch (FACT, previews only, low confidence) [S62][S63]. Carne's own note says a China Mysore room cost about 1,500 a month in 2023 [S141].
- The top published price found for an Ashtanga program in China is 28,000 CNY (FACT) [S76]. The smallest Ashtanga group found is 6 students, in the Kunming one-month course [S71].
- No long, small, high-priced in-depth course like AYBKK's was found in China. This is not confirmed: the sample is WeChat previews only, and no fresh searches could be run. Do not say in public that none exists.

## 4. Fitness and outdoor life

### The picture

Official support goes to outdoor sport, not to studios.

- The prefecture counts 13,120 sports venues: courts, fields and trails. It publishes no count of gyms or yoga studios (FACT, end 2025) [S3].
- 38.7% of people in the prefecture exercise regularly (FACT, 2025) [S87].
- Ten public venues open free or at low cost. They served over 6.53 million visits in 2025 (FACT, prefecture) [S87]. Free public classes in one county include tai chi and baduanjin. Yoga is not on the list (FACT) [S88].
- The prefecture held 108 mass sport events with 110,800 participants in 2025 (FACT) [S88]. Official money figures for these events conflict, so they are left out.
- The plan for outdoor sport wants outdoor participants to equal 18% of all tourists in 2026 and 22% in 2030 (FACT, targets only) [S86]. The 2026 push includes pickleball (FACT) [S88].

Whether each gym is still open was not checked. No public price list for an ordinary Dali gym was found.
