The lake has hard rules. Boats on Erhai are capped. Camping, picnics and fishing in the lake area and the lakeside belt are banned, with fines of 100 to 1,000 CNY (FACT, in force 1 Dec 2023) [S34]. Ask the lake office before any lakeside class.

## 5. The China-wide picture

### Market size

| What | Figure | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Commercial fitness market | 382.7 billion CNY, up 34.2%. The method is not explained. | VENDOR CLAIM, 2025, weak | [S100] |
| Gym members | 84.518 million, down 3.44% | VENDOR CLAIM, 2025 | [S100] |
| Fitness venues | About 120,000, which is 23,000 fewer than 2024. 63,000 of them are "pan-fitness": yoga, Pilates, boxing and martial arts mixed. | VENDOR CLAIM, 2025 | [S105] |
| Fitness rooms in the government survey | 156,300 | FACT, end 2025 | [S107] |
| Yoga market | 38.7 billion CNY in 2020; over 65,000 studios quoted for 2021 | VENDOR CLAIM, old and second-hand | [S106] |
| Yoga studios on Meituan | over 35,000 | FACT, May 2023 | [S39] |
| China's "wellness economy" | US$950 billion. Very broad: food, beauty, fitness, travel. | FACT (an institute's estimate), 2024 | [S118] |

No yoga market figure for 2023 to 2026 from a named report was found.

### Closures and the habit gap

| What | Figure | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Closure rate in "main cities" | Clubs 20.4%; studios 20.7% | VENDOR CLAIM, 2025 | [S101] |
| Club income per month, main cities | 289,000 CNY, down 22.5% | VENDOR CLAIM, 2025 | [S100] |
| Visits per month, all members | 2.63 (2.35 in 2023) | VENDOR CLAIM, 2025 | [S102] |
| Visits per month, members who do show up | 7.65 (4.41 in 2023) | VENDOR CLAIM, 2025 | [S102] |
| Annual cards sold under 3,000 CNY | 57.4% | VENDOR CLAIM, 2025 | [S103] |
| Low-price 24-hour gyms | 4,772, up 52.95%. Month card 99 to 129 CNY at one chain. | VENDOR CLAIM, 2025 | [S104] |

All six rows come from one seller of gym software. They are about gyms, mostly in big cities. They are not about yoga studios and not about Dali. The average member comes fewer than 3 times a month: that is the habit gap in one number. Check it against the full report before any public use.

### Prepaid-card rules

A prepaid card means the buyer pays first and uses the service later.

| Rule | What it says | Where | Kind, date | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| State Council Decree 778 | Any prepaid service needs a written contract. A business in serious trouble must stop taking prepayments. | All China | FACT, in force 1 Jul 2024 | [S110] |
| Supreme People's Court rule | A buyer can ask for the money back within 7 days of paying. There are exceptions, such as a buyer who already had the same service. "No refund" terms are void (they do not count). The exceptions were read on a copy of the text, not on the court's own page. | All China | FACT, in force 1 May 2025 | [S108] |
| Court model case | A closed yoga studio had about 200 members and about 400,000 CNY of unused prepayments. The man who took over the firm and shut it down had to refund a member himself. | All China | FACT, 14 Mar 2025 | [S109] |
| Shanghai rule | Limits per person: membership 5,000 CNY and 24 months; class packs 20,000 CNY and 60 sessions; 20,000 CNY in total. It ends on 31 Dec 2026. Renewal not found. | Shanghai only | FACT, in force 1 Mar 2025 | [S111] |
| Shandong limits and a Shenzhen draft | Similar limits and a 7-day cooling-off period (days in which a buyer may change their mind) | Shandong, Shenzhen | Not confirmed (a vendor's summary only) | [S112] |

No prepaid rule or platform for Yunnan or Dali was found. Read the full court text with a lawyer before changing any contract.

### Spending mood

- Retail sales of consumer goods grew 0.4% in Oct 2026, and 1.0% for January to October. Retail sales of services grew 4.9% in the same release (FACT) [S113].
- Household spending per person grew 3.6% in the first three quarters of 2026. Spending on education, culture and recreation grew 4.6% (FACT) [S114].
- Consumer confidence stood at 89.2 in July 2026. The long-run average is about 108 (FACT, an OECD series on a data site) [S115].
- lululemon's "comparable sales" in China Mainland fell 8% in the quarter to 2 Aug 2026, with exchange-rate moves taken out. Comparable sales set this year against the same shops and channels a year before (FACT; this is clothing, not classes) [S116].

### Healing and long-stay trends

- Long-stay is official policy in Yunnan: over 5.5 million people in 2025, up 41.4% (FACT) [S13].
- Yunnan has a "do and do not" list for long-stay business, in force since 20 Nov 2026. It bans lodging trade without legal papers, exaggerated or false promises about results, and fund-raising under the long-stay name (FACT) [S35].
- A report teaser says stays of 1 to 3 months are the mainstream and monthly budgets cluster at 3,000 to 5,000 CNY. The most wanted activity is wellness therapy and health checks (53.94%), then walking and light exercise (50.83%) (VENDOR CLAIM, Nov 2026; sample size not given) [S117].
- Across China, Meituan searches for "healing" rose 256% in 2023, and for "singing-bowl healing" 423% (FACT, Dec 2023) [S57].
- The size of China's "healing economy" is unknown. One article prints 299.6 billion CNY for 2022. Another prints 2.72 trillion CNY for 2025 with no source. It is a FACT that both are in print. Neither size is confirmed. Use neither [S57][S58].

## 6. Running a workshop or retreat there

### Venues

No hall size, mat count or day rate was found for any Dali venue. These are leads only.

| Venue | Where | What is known | Kind | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Argyle Resort Dali Yueyun | Dali City, development zone | 415 rooms; the listing names "meeting" (2019) | VENDOR CLAIM | [S131] |
| Pullman Dali | Dali City, Xiaguan lakeside | From US$97 a night; no meeting-room data | VENDOR CLAIM | [S130] |
| Linden Centre (喜林苑) | Xizhou and the Old Town | Heritage hotels; no hall data | VENDOR CLAIM | [S132] |
| 六时吉祥 My House hotel | Old Town side | Has hosted trainings; room 160 a person shared, 320 single (2024) | VENDOR CLAIM | [S42] |
| Jingdi Yoga Meditation Hotel | Dali City, Yinqiao town | 9 rooms; looks barely active | VENDOR CLAIM | [S51] |
| Veggie Ark | North of the Old Town | Had yoga studios in 2024; status not checked | FACT | [S24] |
| Shimenguan hot spring hotel | Prefecture: Yangbi County | A guide says it has a yoga room | FACT | [S99] |

Ask each one: floor area, floor type, heating, 6 am access, and whether it can register foreign passports.

### Costs

- Hall hire: not found.
- Room and food marks: 1,000 CNY for 7 days of shared room and 3 meals at a temple retreat (FACT, 2024) [S81]. 2,900 CNY for 15 days in Xishuangbanna (FACT, year not shown) [S85]. 1,200 CNY for shared room and food at a two-week Guangzhou training (FACT, 2022) [S84]. Dali rooms from 200 CNY a night on weekdays (FACT, 2026) [S19].
- Hotels on Trip.com average about US$78 a night. Rates double around Chinese New Year (VENDOR CLAIM, Nov 2026) [S130].
- Group meals: no current price found.
- Travel for two teachers: 2 x US$163 = US$326 return to Kunming, plus 420 to 622 CNY for the train, both ways (ESTIMATE: 4 x 105 and 4 x 155.5, from [S28][S29]).
- Time: 5 teaching days mean 7 days away (ESTIMATE: one travel day each way).

### Altitude and season

- Dali sits at about 1,975 m. That is below the 2,450 m sleeping height where the US health agency CDC says the risk of altitude illness starts. But the CDC adds "and sometimes lower", and advises only mild exercise for the first 48 hours (FACT) [S23][S129]. A careful reading, not medical advice: plan a light first day for people who fly in from sea level.
- June to October is wet. The dry months are cold at 6 am: near 3 C in January. Whether halls have heating is unknown. Ask [S23].
- About 80 days a year are peak days with higher prices [S21].

### Local rules to know

- The lake. No new building within 100 m of Erhai on the west and north shores, or within 30 m of the ring road on the north-east shore, except public works (FACT, set in 2017 to 2018) [S33].
- Guesthouses. A prefecture guesthouse law has been in force since 1 Jan 2026 (FACT) [S32]. The Yunnan list bans lodging trade without legal papers [S35].
- The Old Town. The city ran a street clean-up drive from July to October 2024 against stalls and shops that spread onto the street (FACT) [S36].
- Events have been stopped before. A large digital-nomad conference in Dali was cancelled on 2 Nov 2023, a week before it began. That case was tied to tech work. Nothing like it was found for yoga (FACT) [S37].
- Size. The national permit for large public events starts at 1,000 people. A workshop is far below that (FACT, 2007 text) [S128]. This settles only the national permit. Whether the venue or the local police want notice is for the host to ask.
- Sleeping place. A foreigner who sleeps outside a hotel must register with the police within 24 hours (FACT) [S120].

### Box: visa, work and tax rules for a foreign teacher

> [!warning] I am not a lawyer. This is not legal or tax advice.
>
> **What is law** (texts re-opened on 22 Nov 2026)
>
> - Entry. Thai passport holders may enter China without a visa: 30 days per stay, and no more than 90 days in any 180. This does not cover work (FACT, in force 1 Mar 2024) [S119].
> - Work. A foreigner who works in China needs a work permit and a work-type residence permit. The law does not define "work" and sets no shortest length (FACT) [S120].
> - Visa types. The M visa is for trade, the F visa for exchange and visits, the Z visa for work (FACT, 2013 rule) [S143]. A law-firm guide says the M visa cannot be used for employment (FACT) [S144]. So neither visa-free entry nor an M visa is a work permit.
> - Purpose. Doing things that do not match the stated reason for the stay can bring an order to leave (FACT) [S120].
> - Fines. Illegal work costs the foreigner 5,000 to 20,000 CNY (25,000 to 101,000 THB), with 5 to 15 days of detention in serious cases. Whoever hires pays 10,000 CNY per person, up to 100,000 (FACT) [S120][S133].
> - Bans. Repatriation (being sent home) brings a ban on entry for 1 to 5 years. Deportation brings 10 years (FACT) [S120].
> - Short work. The Bangkok visa centre shows a short-term work approval, for under 90 days, only for commercial shows. It does not say where a paid yoga workshop fits (FACT, read 22 Nov 2026) [S126].
> - Tax treaty (a deal between two countries on who may tax what). A fee for teaching in China that is paid by a resident of China may be taxed in China, even for a 5-day visit. Staying under 183 days does not change this (FACT, 1986 agreement, Article 14; later changes were not checked) [S121].
> - Withholding (the payer keeps the tax back and sends it to the tax office). Income from work done in China counts as China income wherever it is paid. For a visitor, 20% of the fee is taken off, then a rate of 3% to 45% applies. The 45% rate starts above 80,000 CNY taxable in one month. If the payer fails to withhold, the teacher still owes the tax (FACT) [S122][S123][S125].
> - VAT (a sales tax). On a person's service fee it is 1% in 2026 to 2027, and 3% in normal years (FACT, per PwC) [S124].
> - Adverts. Training adverts may not promise results or use student praise. Words such as "national-level", "highest" and "best" are banned in all adverts, with a fine of 200,000 to 1,000,000 CNY for the advertiser (FACT, read on a copy of the law) [S127]. AYBKK's own ad plan reads this as: no 最 (most) and no 第一 (number one) [S134].
>
> **What is my estimate**
>
> | Fee paid by a Chinese host in one month | Income tax withheld | Share of the fee |
> |---|---|---|
> | 50,000 CNY | 7,590 CNY | 15.2% |
> | 100,000 CNY | 20,840 CNY | 20.8% |
> | 200,000 CNY | 56,840 CNY | 28.4% |
>
> The sum: fee x 80% x rate, minus the fixed amount in the tax table. Example: 100,000 x 80% = 80,000. Then 80,000 x 35% minus 7,160 = 20,840. VAT of about 1% comes on top (ESTIMATE from [S123][S124]).
>
> **What is common practice** (no source found)
>
> - The host studio is the legal organiser. It takes the students' money, gives the fapiao (China's official tax receipt), withholds the tax and pays the teacher.
> - Common practice is not a legal shield. Nothing found says this set-up makes a paid teaching visit legal.
>
> **What a Chinese lawyer or the host must confirm**
>
> 1. Does a paid workshop of 3 to 10 days count as "work"? Which entry status does the teacher need?
> 2. Will the host write an invitation that matches what is really done?
> 3. How is the tax worked out, who bears it, and will the host hand over the tax certificate (proof that the tax was paid)?
> 4. Has a later change altered Article 14 of the treaty? How does Thailand give credit for tax paid in China? This one is for a Thai accountant.
> 5. Who files the VAT, and who gives fapiao to students?
> 6. Do the venue or the local police want notice of an event led by a foreigner?
> 7. If students pay a Thai account from abroad, the tax picture may differ. Ask. Do not assume.

## 7. Options for AYBKK

Numbers in this section come from sections 1 to 6, where each one has its kind and date. New sums here are ESTIMATES, and each shows its sum.

### Two tools, in plain words

- **Hormozi's value equation.** An offer is worth more when the dream outcome is big and the buyer believes it will work. It is worth less when the wait is long and the effort is high. As a sum: value = (dream outcome x belief it will work) / (time delay x effort and sacrifice).
- **Munger's inversion.** Do not ask "how do I win?" first. Ask "what would kill this?" Then stay away from those things.

### The base sums

- Goal: about 33 million THB a year net (net is what is left after costs). This is the planning files' own guess [S134].
- One full cohort: 12 x 180,000 = 2,160,000 THB. That is 6.5% of the goal, before costs [S134][S135].
- One cohort teaching day: 2,160,000 / 30 = 72,000 THB, or about 14,200 CNY (ESTIMATE at 5.066) [S133].
- One seat: 180,000 THB, or about 35,500 CNY (ESTIMATE at 5.066) [S133].
- The goal would need about 15 full cohorts a year. The plan says: "One teacher can't teach that. That is the real wall." [S134]
- The planning files used a round 5 THB per CNY. This report uses today's 5.066, so a few results differ a little.

### Comparison

| Option | What it is | Money (ESTIMATE) | Days away | What could kill it | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A. Do nothing in Dali, for now | No trip, no offer. Fill cohort 3. Settle the visa question. | Cost 0. Protects 2,160,000 THB. | 0 | "Not now" turns into "never look" | Yes. The default until 9 Feb 2027. |
| B. City workshop with a host | 5 days, same form as the 2026 city stops | Gross 49,200 to 102,500 CNY, or 1.4 to 2.9 seats, before [AYBKK share], tax and hall | 7 | No host with papers; work rules; small base | No, until a host brings a room, papers and paid deposits |
| C. Yearly retreat people travel to | 5 or 6 days, room and practice in one price | Gross 95,988 to 159,980 CNY. My guess for net: 1 to 3 seats a year. | 8, plus weeks of planning | Most visible form of paid teaching; a new product | No for 2027 |
| D. Part of a 180K cohort in Dali | Move some of the 30 days | Income the same. Cost and risk go up. | the cohort days | A ban on entry ends every China stop | No |
| E. Dali as content only | 2 posts that ask "which city do you practise in?" | Cash 0 | 0 | Pulls tourists, not habit builders | Yes, small. Read on 11 Dec. |
| F. Bring the Dali kind of person to Bangkok | One month of Mysore in Bangkok, on the 1 month pass | 10,272 THB a pass with VAT. 10 students = 0.53 seat. | 0 | Small numbers | Yes, as the one action under E |
| G. If Yunnan at all, Kunming first | Ask Kunming before Dali | Heads x 4,100 CNY. Heads unknown. | about 6 | No host known; same work rules | Not yet. Same gate as B. |

### A. Do nothing in Dali, for now

- Sum. Cost 0. One more paid seat is worth 35% to 72% of the gross of a whole Dali workshop (ESTIMATE: 35,530 / 102,500 and 35,530 / 49,200).
- Value equation, for Boonchu and not for a student. Outcome: the yearly goal. Belief: higher when his days go to the offer that already sells. Delay: none. Effort: none added. Doing nothing new is a real choice.
- What could kill it. Someone else takes "serious Ashtanga in Dali": that has already happened, with a daily Mysore room since Oct 2021 [S62] and at least seven organisers (section 3). A graduate in Yunnan asks to host and gets no answer. "Not now" turns into "never look". The visa question gets dropped along with Dali, when it hangs over every China stop.

### B. A normal city workshop in Dali, with a host

- Price. Hefei regular full price: 4,100 CNY (20,800 THB) [S139][S133]. The Xi'an price in 2024 was 3,680 CNY for 6 days [S82].
- Heads. Ashtanga events in Dali show 12 places and caps of 20 to 25 [S67][S65]. AYBKK's own stops: the Hefei list has 48 full rows plus 14 part rows (private number) [S138]. Huizhou had 43 [S137].
- Gross. 12 x 4,100 = 49,200 CNY. 25 x 4,100 = 102,500 CNY. The Hefei ceiling is 48 x 4,100 = 196,800 CNY. Dali is 25% to 52% of that.
- In seats. 49,200 x 5.066 = about 249,000 THB = 1.4 seats. 102,500 x 5.066 = about 519,000 THB = 2.9 seats. That is 0.8% to 1.6% of the goal, even gross (ESTIMATE).
- Net = gross x [AYBKK share], minus Chinese tax (my estimate: 15.2% on 50,000 CNY, 20.8% on 100,000 CNY, plus about 1% VAT), minus what the days away cost the Bangkok shala.
- Per day away. 49,200 / 7 = 7,029 CNY. 102,500 / 7 = 14,643 CNY. A cohort day is about 14,200 CNY. So the best case only matches a cohort day, and that is before the host's share and tax.
- For the buyer. 4,100 CNY is about one "comfortable" month of living in Dali (rough blog figure) [S17]. It is 2.7 months of the local Mysore room at 1,500 CNY [S61].
- Value equation. Dream: get unstuck and build a daily practice, with a visiting teacher from a working Mysore shala. Belief: medium. Chinese students can already choose among 45 listed teachers in China and Hong Kong, plus many visiting ones [S59]. What makes a visitor stand out is a working shala that students can go and see. Delay: five days. And for once there is a daily Mysore room in town to carry on in. That is the best point for B. Effort: little travel for locals, but the price is a month of local living.
- What could kill it:
  1. No host. No graduate is known in Dali. A studio without proper papers falls under Yunnan's new long-stay list [S35].
  2. Work rules. See the box in section 6. The same risk sits at every China stop.
  3. Small base. One listed room, class size unknown. Events of 12 to 25.
  4. Local wallets. Urban spending in the prefecture is 34,344 CNY a year per person [S3].
  5. Season. The 2026 tour ran in April and June to August [S136]. June to October is Dali's rain. Summer rooms near the Old Town cost 30% to 50% more [S20]. The dry months clash with cohort 3 and with cold mornings.
  6. Altitude. Plan a light first day.
  7. Day budget. 90 visa-free days in any 180 [S119]. The 2026 pages already show 33 teaching days in China from April to August [S136].
  8. Host words. Hosts write their own posters and titles [S82]. The advert law bans words such as "highest" and "best" [S127]. Give each host one approved line.
  9. Prepaid rules. A buyer can ask for the money back within 7 days [S108]. Early-bird cash is less sure than it looks.
  10. The "healing" label. Dali yoga weeks get gong baths and singing-bowl certificates added [S67][S64]. That fights "from trend to habit" [S142].
  11. Manners. Teaching in a town with one listed room, without that teacher's welcome.

### C. A yearly retreat in Dali that people travel to

- Price marks. 7,999 to 8,999 CNY for 6 days with lodging [S67]. 7,900 to 8,800 CNY for 5 days with room and food [S66]. 4,880 CNY for 7 days [S68].
- Gross. 12 x 7,999 = 95,988 CNY. 20 x 7,999 = 159,980 CNY.
- Room and food inside that price: about 1,000 to 1,200 CNY a head for 6 days (ESTIMATE from three marks). The marks: 1,000 for 7 days at a temple retreat [S81]; 2,900 / 15 x 6 = 1,160 in Xishuangbanna [S85]; a Dali room at 200 or more a night, shared by two [S19].
- Left before hall, host share and tax. 12 heads: 95,988 minus 12,000 to 14,400 = about 82,000 to 84,000 CNY, or 2.3 to 2.4 seats. 20 heads: 159,980 minus 20,000 to 24,000 = about 136,000 to 140,000 CNY, or 3.8 to 3.9 seats.
- My guess for net: 1 to 3 seats a year. That is 180,000 to 540,000 THB, or 0.5% to 1.6% of the goal.
- Value equation. Dream: a week with the teacher in a beautiful place. The mountain and the lake do half the selling, so it pulls trip buyers more than habit builders. Belief: Dali weeks carry the "healing" label, which the press treats with doubt [S45]. Delay: one week, then home to no room. That is the very gap AYBKK sells against [S142]. Effort: the highest of all options. A week of leave, 8,000 to 9,000 CNY plus travel, and two slow days at altitude.
- What could kill it. It is the most visible form of paid teaching by a foreigner: a public advert across China. Place rules: the guesthouse law, the Yunnan list, the lake rules [S32][S35][S34]. A crowded field of at least seven organisers. Rooms double at Chinese New Year [S130]. Deposits go to a venue while buyers can ask for refunds within 7 days [S108]. A retreat is a trip, not a habit. It is a new product, and the plan says "nothing new after 12 Dec" [S134]. A large fee in one month climbs the tax table [S123].
- If a paid week that people travel to is wanted, build it in Thailand, where the work rules are not in question and the Bangkok room is the proof.

### D. Hold part of a future 180K cohort in Dali

- Sum. Income does not change: 12 x 180,000 = 2,160,000 THB in Bangkok or in Dali. Only cost and risk change.
- Added. Hall hire (no rate found). Travel and rooms for two teachers. Possible Chinese tax on fees paid by Chinese residents (ESTIMATE: 28.4% on a 200,000 CNY fee = 56,840 CNY). Weeks of paid teaching look like work. Each Dali day comes out of the 90 visa-free days.
- Saved, for students only. One flight: Kunming to Bangkok from US$82 to 86 one way [S28]. They did not ask for it.
- Value equation. Belief is lower in Dali, because the proof is the Bangkok room and its regulars. Effort is lower for Chinese students: no passport, own language. That is the only gain.
- What could kill it. A ban on entry. Tax withheld on a large fee. Winter: cohort 3 runs 11 Jan to 9 Feb, when Dali mornings are near 3 C [S23]. A month of strong practice at 1,975 m. No room of his own. The Bangkok shala loses its teachers. New signed terms are needed.

### E. Dali as a content and audience angle only

- What. No trip. Use Dali as a story, and as a way to find serious practitioners who live or long-stay in Dali and Yunnan. One action per post: name your city. Plato drafts. Boonchu posts. No ads.
- Sum. Cash 0. Pool: nearly 200,000 long-stay people in Dali City in the first half of 2026 [S8]. Serious Ashtanga base: tens of people, not hundreds (ESTIMATE: my reading of two rooms and events of 12 to 25). Payoff = [Yunnan names] x [share who apply] x [share who pay] x 180,000 THB. One seat equals 18.75 Bangkok month passes at 9,600 THB (180,000 / 9,600) [S137].
- Value equation, for the viewer. Dream: a practice that outlasts the stay. Belief: plain facts, no promises. Delay: they can start tomorrow morning in the Mysore room where they live. Effort: follow, and name a city.
- What could kill it. The wrong crowd: Dali pictures pull tourists and trip buyers. Unchecked numbers in public. "Healing" words creeping in. It can look like fishing in the Dali teacher's pond: ask her first and point people to her room. It steals post slots from cohort 3: cap it at 2 posts.

### F. Bring the Dali kind of person to Bangkok

- What. Dali's long-stayers already move for one to three months and work from a laptop. Offer them the next stop: one month of Mysore in Bangkok, in the room where the 180K course is taught. It uses the 1 month pass and the Chinese welcome page that already exist [S137][S136]. No new product. No teaching inside China.
- Sum. 9,600 THB + 7% VAT = 10,272 THB [S137]. Train from Dali to Kunming: 105 to 155.5 CNY [S29]. Kunming to Bangkok: 2 h 21 min, from US$163 return [S28]. Entry to Thailand: 30 days visa-free for a visit, not for work; check the current Thai rule [S119]. Ten such students a year = 96,000 THB before VAT = 0.53 of one seat. Small by itself. The value is the missing paid step below 180K.
- Value equation. Dream: a month in the teacher's own room. Belief: high, because it is the working shala. Delay: 30 days give a habit a real start. A week does not. Effort: a flight and a month of Bangkok rent. They already live this way.
- What could kill it. Small numbers. Bangkok costs more than Dali, where rooms run 500 to 2,000 CNY a month [S15]. It grows into a new product with its own page and admin: keep it to one line under the posts. Who answers Chinese visitors on WeChat? Entry rules can change.

### G. If Yunnan at all, Kunming before Dali

- Why. Kunming has the nonstop flight from Bangkok [S28], more Ashtanga rooms [S73], and a Mysore room that already hosts visiting listed teachers [S72].
- Sum. Five teaching days mean about 6 days away, not 7. Meituan listed over 300 yoga studios in Kunming in 2023 [S39]. One Kunming room charged 4,600 CNY list for a 5-day visitor in 2024 [S72]. It also ran a one-month course for 6 students [S71]. Dali people can come by train in about 2 hours for 105 to 155.5 CNY [S29]. Gross = heads x 4,100 CNY. Heads unknown.
- Value equation. The same as B, with a shorter trip and a bigger city base. Less scenery, which is fine: students come for the room.
- What could kill it. No graduate or host is known there either. The same work-rule, tax and day-budget risks as B. Unproven headcount.

## 8. Recommendation

**The plan in one sentence:** fill cohort 3, settle the visa and tax question once for every China stop, and let Dali prove itself on paper before it costs a teaching day.

In short: A for teaching. E as a 19-day test with no trip, with F as its one action. B only if the test passes, as one stop on a tour. If Yunnan, ask Kunming first (G). C and D: no.

The real find is not Dali. Two things hang over every China stop: the work rules and the tax on a host-paid fee. Fix those first.

### Steps

| # | Step | Owner | Day |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Count how many 2026 workshop students wrote a Yunnan city on the orientation form. For Xichang, also count those from outside Sichuan. Counts only, no names [S136]. | Neo | by Sun 29 Nov 2026 |
| 2 | Two private WeChat messages. (a) To the Xi'an studio that later sold a Dali and Lijiang week: how many came? (b) To the teacher of the daily Mysore room in Dali: how many practise each morning, and would a visit be welcome? | Boonchu | by Sun 29 Nov 2026 |
| 3 | One post: "the room, not the view". It ends with "Which city do you practise in?" No offer, no price, no date. | Plato drafts; Boonchu posts after his "go" | week of 30 Nov 2026 |
| 4 | One hour with a Chinese immigration lawyer. Take the 7 questions from the box in section 6. | Boonchu | Boonchu to pick the day. My suggestion: before Fri 11 Dec 2026. |
| 5 | One approved Chinese line for how hosts describe the teachers, sent to every host. | Carne drafts; Boonchu approves | Boonchu to pick the day. My suggestion: by Fri 11 Dec 2026. |
| 6 | Read the test once. Pass or kill. | Boonchu, with Machi | Fri 11 Dec 2026 |
| 7 | Only if steps 1 to 3 look good: the host takes fully refundable deposits in the host's own WeChat, with written terms, for 14 days. | The host | to Fri 25 Dec 2026 |

**Pass (all three):** a named host with a business licence and a room; 15 or more Yunnan names across steps 1 and 3 (my number, change it); and [X] deposits. X is the headcount that makes a stop worth a tour day: X = ([net per workshop day] x [days away]) / (4,100 CNY x [AYBKK share]).

**Kill (any one):** fewer than 5 Yunnan names in AYBKK's own data (my number); the Xi'an studio's Dali week did not fill; no host by 11 Dec. Then Dali goes on the "not in 2027" list, and nobody raises it before 9 Feb 2027.

If B ever passes: 9 to 19 April 2027 is the one family-free block in Dali's dry season [S135]. It ends before March Street, which should fall around 21 to 27 April 2027 (ESTIMATE). Not checked: Songkran, and who runs the shala.

### What we know, think, and may be wrong about

| We know (FACT) | We think (ESTIMATE) | We may be wrong about |
|---|---|---|
| One teacher in Dali is on the Sharath Yoga Centre list of 45 for China [S59] | The serious Ashtanga base in Dali is tens of people, not hundreds | The Dali room may be bigger than the events suggest. Its class size was not found. |
| Ashtanga events in Dali show 12 places and caps of 20 to 25 [S67][S65] | A 5-day Dali workshop grosses 1.4 to 2.9 seats of the 180K | A cohort graduate may live in Yunnan and want to host |
| Visa-free entry does not cover work. The fine is 5,000 to 20,000 CNY. Repatriation brings a ban for 1 to 5 years [S119][S120] | Chinese tax on a host-paid fee is about 15% to 28%, plus about 1% VAT | A short paid workshop may not count as "work". Only a lawyer can say. |
| No direct Bangkok to Dali flight shows in Nov 2026 [S26][S27] | Five teaching days cost 7 days away | The direct flight may come back |
| The long-stay counts are large, and they disagree [S7][S9][S10][S11][S12] | Long-stayers are mostly passers-by on small budgets | Xichang may have drawn many students who flew in. Then B gets a fair hearing. |
| Cohort 3 has 12 seats at 180,000 THB and runs 11 Jan to 9 Feb 2027 [S135] | The best Dali case only matches one cohort teaching day | How many cohort 3 seats are paid. It is not in any file read [S134]. |

### Questions only Boonchu can answer

1. Does any graduate of cohort 1 or 2 live in Yunnan? Has anyone asked to host in Dali or Kunming? If yes, do they have a room and a business licence?
2. Do you know the teacher who runs the daily Mysore room in Dali? Would she welcome a visit, or see it as stepping on her room?
3. What does a normal China stop net per day away, after the host's share and the travel days? For Xichang, 24 to 28 June 2026: how many came, how many flew in from another province, and what did it net per day?
4. How many of the 12 seats in cohort 3 are paid today?
5. Which entry status was used for the 2026 China workshops? Has a Chinese immigration lawyer, or a host's exit-entry office (the police office that handles foreigners' stays), ever confirmed in writing that it covers paid teaching?
6. Who pays for a China workshop: the host as a fee, or the students to a Thai account? Was Chinese tax withheld, and are the tax certificates on file?
7. At its tightest point, how many of the 90 visa-free days in 180 did the 2026 tour use? Who runs the Bangkok shala when both teachers travel?
8. Is Dali a business idea, or a place you and the family want to be? Both are fine. They need different sums.

**One closing question, yes or no:** do we park Dali until 9 Feb 2027 and run only the 19-day paper test?

## 9. Content angles for Plato (ideas only)

Drafts only. Each needs Boonchu's "go" and a Chinese reader check before posting. No 最, no 第一, no "healing" words, no politics, no price.

1. **"The room, not the view"** (story Reel, and the test post). Hook: "I looked at Dali. Mountain, lake, clean air. I stayed in my room in Bangkok." Chinese draft: "我看过大理。山，湖，好空气。我还是留在了曼谷的教室。" Turn: "A view is new for a week. A teacher who is there every morning stays new for years." One action: "Which city do you practise in? Tell me." Guard: no knock on Dali or on retreats, no numbers.
2. **"A retreat is a trip. Tuesday at 6 is a practice."** Chinese draft: "旅修是一趟旅行。周二早上六点，才是练习。" Say what a week away gives (a start), then what it misses (the Tuesday after). One action: "What time is your mat tomorrow? Write it below." Guard: never say retreats do not work. Show the gap only. No promised result.
3. **"Your desk moved to Dali. Your practice can move too."** For long-stay and remote workers. Chinese draft: "工位搬到了大理，练习也可以跟着走。" Same sequence, same hour, any room. Next stop, in one line: a month in the Bangkok room. One action: "Find the morning Mysore room in your town. Go once this week." Guard: name the Dali room only with that teacher's OK. No day counts or visitor numbers until the source is opened and checked.
4. **"Breath first, then bend: your first mornings in a high town."** It reuses the Xichang page line "呼吸静定" [S136]. Day one in a high town: do less. Count the breath, not the poses. One action: "Tomorrow: half the practice, full breath." Guard: practice advice only, no health claim, no altitude numbers. Add: "If you feel unwell, stop and see a doctor."
5. **For studio owners and teachers (the 180K): "Your students come for a season."** Chinese draft: "你的学员来一季，就走。" Every studio now has its own long-stay crowd: three months, then gone. The skill is being the teacher who notices on day 8 that one is missing. One action: "Owners and teachers: message me on WeChat about the in-depth course." Guard: no promised result, no student numbers, no student praise, and never 认证 or 授权 for the course.
